**Catalogue delivery (2026-11-02):** SLAPP-79/80 (Cafe Catalogue & Glofox Seed). (1) Endpoints under `/api/v1/cafe`: Categories CRUD (`GET /categories` with `itemCount`, `POST /categories`, `PATCH /categories/:categoryId`, `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` with 409 `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS` guard); Items CRUD (`GET /items` with pagination, search, category filter, member public isolation; `POST /items`; `GET /items/:itemId`; `PATCH /items/:itemId`; `PATCH /items/:itemId/availability`; `PATCH /items/:itemId/visibility`; `POST /uploads/image/token`). Items are never hard-deleted (`DELETE` returns 405). Reads allowed for OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER; writes allowed for OWNER, ADMIN only. Rate limited via `cafeReadLimiter` on reads and `cafeLimiter` on writes/uploads. (2) Sold-out business logic: tracked items (`isTracked=true`) require `stockQuantity` and `unitCost`, auto-set `sold_out` when stock reaches 0, manual setting to `available` with 0 stock returns 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`; untracked items (made to order: Coffee, Protein Shakes, Protein Coolers) force stock and unitCost to null with manual availability; sold-out items remain visible to members. (3) Glofox seed: `prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js` reads sheet "Store" from `docs/Spirit_Level_Glofox_Business_Data.xlsx` (61 items, 12 categories, 18 untracked, 23 tracked sold out, 20 tracked available, 7 staff_only); supports `--dry-run` and `--force` (default skips items that already exist by `externalRef`; `--force` overwrites them). (4) Data model & Migration: `20261102130000_add_cafe_catalogue` + reversible `down.sql`: creates `cafe_categories` and `cafe_items` tables with CHECK constraints, foreign key `onDelete: Restrict`, and lowercase API / uppercase DB enum mapping. (5) Web/mobile parity: standard envelopes, Bearer JWT, no Origin-dependent behavior. (6) OpenAPI: all 11 cafe operations (plus their 405 handlers) are documented in `docs/openapi.yaml` under the `Cafe` tag. (7) Tests: see section 23 for the final cafe suite counts.

## 1. Stack & Conventions

Node ≥ 20 / Express 5, CommonJS (`require`/`module.exports`), plain JavaScript, function-based modules, async/await throughout. **No TypeScript remains** (verified: no `.ts`/`.d.ts` files, no `tsconfig`, no `ts-node` or `@types/*` in `package.json`).
Prisma 6 + PostgreSQL (Neon). Zod v4 for runtime validation. bcrypt for passwords. pino + pino-http for structured logging.
Runtime deps (`package.json`): `@prisma/client`, `@vercel/blob`, `bcrypt`, `cors`, `dotenv`, `express`, `express-rate-limit`, `helmet`, `jsonwebtoken`, `node-cron`, `nodemailer`, `pino`, `pino-http`, `swagger-ui-express`, `yaml`, `zod`. Dev deps: `jest` 30, `supertest`, `eslint` 10 (flat config) + `@eslint/js` + `eslint-config-prettier` + `globals`, `prettier`, `nodemon`, `pino-pretty`, `prisma`, `exceljs` (seed only). `multer` is **not** installed (removed after the client-upload refactor). Node `crypto` is used for UUIDs, refresh tokens, temp passwords and SHA-256 hashing.
npm scripts: `test` (`jest --runInBand`), `dev` (`nodemon src/server.js`), `start` (`node src/server.js`), `lint` (`eslint .`), `lint:fix`, `format` (`prettier --write .`), `prisma:generate`, `prisma:migrate` (`prisma migrate dev`), `prisma:seed` (`node prisma/seed.js`, owner seed), `seed:membership-plans` (`node prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js`), `seed:classes` (`node prisma/seeds/classes.seed.js`), `seed:cafe` (`node prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js`, flags `--dry-run` / `--force`). Windows note: commands run with `npx.cmd` on the developer machine (on Linux use `npx`).
All env vars are validated at startup via Zod in `src/config/env.js`; the process exits on invalid config.
All user-facing validation/error text lives in `src/constants/messages.js` (one constant per distinct message, no duplicate texts); `auth.validation.js` has no inline message literals.
**Layout (layer-based MVC, since 2026-10-10):** `src/modules/` no longer exists. Files live by layer: `controllers/` (HTTP handlers), `services/` (business logic + `auth.guards`, `login.policies`, `staff.policies`), `repositories/` (Prisma data access + `user.mapper`, `staff.mapper`, `membershipPlan.mapper`), `validators/` (Zod schemas, `*.validation.js`), `routes/` (all route files incl. `auth/staff/uploads.routes.js`). File names are unchanged; the §3 headings below group rows by feature but every path is the real current path.
Patterns in use: Strategy (`login.policies`, `staff.policies`, `coach.access` role rules and `UPDATE_FIELDS_BY_ROLE`, all `Map`-based or frozen tables, deny by default), Chain of Responsibility (`auth.guards`), thin controllers, repositories as the only Prisma callers (plus `prisma.$transaction` in `staff.service`).
See [docs/ONBOARDING.md](./ONBOARDING.md) for full architectural rationale and decisions (`README.md` only contains the title "Backend").

## 2. Request Flow

```
HTTP → helmet → cors (origin allow-list) → clientType (sets req.clientType) → pino-http (logs clientType) → express.json (1mb) → express.urlencoded → route
     → authLimiter (auth routes) → validateBody(schema) → controller → service → repository → Prisma
                                                                        ↓ throw / next(err)
                                                        errorHandler (4-arg Express middleware)
Unmatched routes → notFound → errorHandler
```

Login flow detail (`POST /api/v1/auth/login`): see §9 "Implemented login flow".

Staff creation flow (`POST /api/v1/admin/staff`): `authenticate` (Bearer JWT, DB role/status) → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `validateCreateStaff` → `staff.controller.createStaff` → `staff.service.createStaff` → repositories. Not behind `authLimiter`. Response 201 `{ success, message: STAFF_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { ...staffUser, emailSent } }`; `tempPassword` is never in the response.

Coach flows: `POST /api/v1/admin/staff/coaches`: `authenticate` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `validateCreateCoach` → `staff.controller.createCoach` → `staff.service.createCoach` → shared `registerStaffUser` (role policy, duplicate-email check = 409 `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS`, temp password, ONE transaction: User + Coach + `STAFF_CREATED` audit, post-commit invitation email). Response 201 `{ success, message: COACH_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { ...fullCoachView, emailSent } }`. Reads and updates under `/api/v1/coaches`: `authenticate` → `authorize(roles)` → per-role validation (`validateByRole`: query or body schema chosen from `req.user.role`) → `coach.controller` → `coach.service` (ownership, visibility, editable fields via `coach.access`) → `coach.repository`. Query is validated into `req.validatedQuery` (Express 5 `req.query` is read-only).

Membership flows (`/api/v1/memberships`):

- List (`GET /api/v1/memberships`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `membership.controller.listMemberships` → `membership.service.listMemberships` → `membership.repository.listWithPlans` (single Prisma query with `include: { plans: true }`, calculates `planCount = plans.length`, maps plans via `toMembershipPlanResponse`) → 200 `{ success, data: [{ id, name, description, createdAt, planCount, plans }] }`.
- Creation (`POST /api/v1/memberships`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validateCreateMembership` (Zod: trimmed `name` required, `description` optional) → `membership.controller.createMembership` → `membership.service.createMembership` (case-insensitive duplicate check via repository, catches P2002 race on raw SQL `LOWER(name)` index → 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`) → `membership.repository.create` → 201 `{ success, message: MEMBERSHIP_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { id, name, description, createdAt } }`.
- Other methods: `router.all` on `/api/v1/memberships` returns 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow: GET, POST`.

Plan list flow (`GET /api/v1/plans`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `validateListPlansQuery` (Zod, 422, unknown params stripped, result on `req.validatedQuery`) → `plan.controller.listPlans` → `plan.service.listPlans` (list query and the independent `meta.counts` query run in parallel via `Promise.all`; rows go through `toMembershipPlanResponse` then `pick(PLAN_LIST_ITEM_FIELDS)`) → `membershipPlan.repository` (`buildListWhere` + `listByColumn` or `listByNameOrder`, `countNonArchivedByCreditType`). Response 200 `{ success, data, meta }`.

Plan creation flow (`POST /api/v1/plans`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `validateCreatePlan` (Zod; strips helper fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc`; checks mutual exclusivity between `membershipId` and `membership { name, description }` → 422 if both present) → `plan.controller.createPlan` → `plan.service.createPlan` (3 creation modes: Mode 1 existing membership verified via `membership.repository.findById`, else 422 `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND`; Mode 2 new membership + plan + audit row created in ONE atomic Prisma `$transaction` via `createPlanWithAudit`, catches duplicate membership name → 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`; Mode 3 standalone with `membershipId: null`) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/plans/:id`.

Plan detail and edit flows: `GET /api/v1/plans/:id`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)` → `planReadLimiter` → `loadPlan()` (404) → `plan.controller.getPlan` → `plan.service.getPlan` (returns full record incl. `membershipId`, nested `membership: { id, name, description }` or null, `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`, `joiningFee`, `creditSummary`, `creditNote`, `activeClientCount`). `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `loadPlan({ rejectArchived: true })` (404, 409 `plan_archived`) → `validateUpdatePlanBody` (422; rejects non-editable fields incl. `joiningFee`, rejects unknown fields incl. `membership` object, sets `req.validatedBody`) → `plan.controller.updatePlan` → `plan.service.updatePlan` (validates `membershipId` if sent: UUID verified to exist, else 422 `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND`; `null` reassigns to standalone; diff, `duplicate_name`, `plan_in_use`) → `updatePlanWithAudit` (one transaction; DB trigger bumps the version). `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id/status`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `planLimiter` → `loadPlan()` → `validatePlanStatusBody` (422) → `plan.controller.changePlanStatus` → `plan.service.changePlanStatus` (same-status shortcut, `PLAN_STATUS_TRANSITIONS`, restore-time name check; status changes never touch client assignments or memberships) → `changePlanStatusWithAudit` (one transaction, compare-and-set on the old status). `router.all` after the real routes answers 405 + `Allow` for every other method on `/:id` and `/:id/status`.

Class flows (`/api/v1/classes`):

- Listing (`GET /`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `classReadLimiter` → `validateListClassesQuery` (Zod, 422 on bad values; unknown stripped, result on `req.validatedQuery`) → `class.controller.listClasses` → `class.service.listClasses` (role-based filter: members see only active public; coaches see active public + assigned; staff default excludes archived, support status=active/deactivated/archived/all; legacy `active` query param mapped cleanly; batch loads with `CLASS_INCLUDE` and parallel `count`, no N+1) → `class.mapper.toClassResponse`.
- Detail (`GET /:id`): `authenticate` → `authorize(all roles)` → `classReadLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.getClass` → `class.service.getClass` (role visibility check: 404 for member if private or inactive; 404 for coach if private/inactive and unassigned; enriched with scheduleSummary, recurringSchedule, upcomingInstancesSummary) → `toClassResponse`.
- Creation (`POST /`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `validateCreateClass` (422; requires intensity 1-5, validates classType, classPassEnabled, coachIds; public requires >=1 eligible plan; plans checked active and existing) → `class.controller.createClass` → `class.service.createClass` (pre-checks name uniqueness against active classes, catches P2002 race → 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`, atomic class + coach + plan links in `$transaction`) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/classes/:id`.
- Update (`PATCH /:id`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `validateUpdateClass` (rejects non-editable `status`, `active`, `eligiblePlanIds`, `externalRef` and unknown keys with 422; merged state validation prevents DB check violations) → `class.controller.updateClass` → `class.service.updateClass` (rejects inactive or archived class → 409 `CLASS_INACTIVE`; private->public requires >=1 plan; name change uniqueness check + P2002 mapping; optimistic locking with `version` if provided → 409 `CLASS_VERSION_CONFLICT`) → `class.repository.updateClass` (executes set-based SQL UPDATE propagation for capacity and duration to future non-overridden sessions, updates coach links, bumps version).
- Deactivate (`POST /:id/deactivate`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.deactivateClass` → `class.service.deactivateClass` (idempotent if already deactivated; calls `countFutureBookedSessions` interim seam: throws 409 `CLASS_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS` with `details: { sessionCount }` if > 0; sets `status=DEACTIVATED`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'deactivated', active: false }`.
- Activate (`POST /:id/activate`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.activateClass` → `class.service.activateClass` (idempotent if already active; pre-checks active name collision and catches P2002 race → 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`; sets `status=ACTIVE`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'active', active: true }`.
- Archive (`POST /:id/archive`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `class.controller.archiveClass` → `class.service.archiveClass` (idempotent if already archived; calls future bookings seam; sets `status=ARCHIVED`) → 200 `{ id, status: 'archived', active: false }`.
- Eligibility (`PUT /:id/eligibility`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `classLimiter` → `loadClass` (404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`) → `validateUpdateEligibility` (422 body `{ planIds: [] }`) → `class.controller.updateEligibility` → `class.service.updateEligibility` (rejects inactive or archived class → 409 `CLASS_INACTIVE`; public with empty list → 422; validates plans exist and not archived; atomic transaction with `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, replaces links) → 200 `{ classId, planIds }`.
- Other methods: `router.all` on `/`, `/:id`, `/:id/deactivate`, `/:id/activate`, `/:id/archive`, `/:id/eligibility` returns 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow` header.

Cafe catalogue flows (`/api/v1/cafe`):

- Categories list (`GET /categories`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `cafeCategory.controller.getCategories` → `cafeCategory.service.getCategories` (counts public items only for MEMBER role, all items for staff) → 200 `{ success, data: [{ id, name, description, itemCount, createdAt, updatedAt }] }`.
- Category creation (`POST /categories`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCreateCategory` → `cafeCategory.controller.createCategory` → `cafeCategory.service.createCategory` (case-insensitive duplicate check → 409 `CATEGORY_EXISTS`) → 201 + created category.
- Category update & delete (`PATCH`, `DELETE /categories/:categoryId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCategoryIdParam` → update (`validateUpdateCategory`, 409 duplicate check) / delete (409 `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS` guard if any item references category).
- Items list (`GET /items`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateListItemsQuery` → `cafeItem.controller.listItems` → `cafeItem.service.listItems` (escaped search, category filter, member sees only `visibility=public` while sold_out items remain visible) → `toCafeItemResponse` (lowercase API enums, numbers for Decimals, nested category). Response 200 `{ success, data, pagination }`.
- Item creation (`POST /items`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCreateItem` → `cafeItem.controller.createItem` → `cafeItem.service.createItem` (tracked requires stock and unitCost, stock 0 forces `sold_out`, setting `available` with 0 stock -> 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`, untracked forces stock/cost to null) → 201 + `Location: /api/v1/cafe/items/:id`.
- Item detail (`GET /items/:itemId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(all roles)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `cafeItem.controller.getItem` → `cafeItem.service.getItemById` (member querying `staff_only` item gets 404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`) → 200 `{ success, data }`.
- Item core update (`PATCH /items/:itemId`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `validateUpdateItem` (rejects `availability` and `visibility` with 422 `ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE`) → `cafeItem.controller.patchItem` → `cafeItem.service.updateItem` (if tracked stock becomes 0, auto-sets `sold_out`).
- Item availability & visibility updates: `PATCH /items/:itemId/availability`: `cafeLimiter` → `validateUpdateItemAvailability` → `patchItemAvailability` → `cafeItem.service.updateItemAvailability` (setting `available` with 0 stock -> 400). `PATCH /items/:itemId/visibility`: `cafeLimiter` → `validateUpdateItemVisibility` → `patchItemVisibility` → `cafeItem.service.updateItemVisibility`.
- Item stock (`PATCH /items/:itemId/stock`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateItemIdParam` → `validateUpdateItemStock` (required integer `quantity` >= 0, no string coercion -> 422) → `cafeInventory.controller.patchItemStock` → `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` (404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`, 409 `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED`, direct overwrite, `SOLD_OUT` in the same write at 0, restock keeps availability) → 200 `{ success, message: ITEM_STOCK_UPDATED_SUCCESS, data: toCafeStockUpdateResponse view }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: PATCH`.
- Inventory list (`GET /inventory`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `validateListInventoryQuery` (search/categoryId/page/limit as the items list plus uppercase `status`; invalid -> 422) → `cafeInventory.controller.listInventory` → `cafeInventory.service.listInventory` (tracked only incl. `staff_only`, name ASC, status filtered before paging) → 200 `{ success, data: toCafeInventoryItemResponse[], pagination }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: GET`.
- Inventory summary (`GET /inventory/summary`, SLAPP-79/81): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeReadLimiter` → `cafeInventory.controller.getInventorySummary` → `cafeInventory.service.getSummary` (tracked only incl. `staff_only`; no query) → 200 `{ success, data: { trackedItems, lowStock, outOfStock, stockValueAed, itemsMissingCost, itemsSold: null, revenueFromItemsAed: null, bestSeller: null, salesDataAvailable: false } }`. Other methods -> 405 `Allow: GET`.
- Item image upload token (`POST /uploads/image/token`): `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → `validateCafeImageToken` (Zod `image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp` -> 422 on bad MIME) → `issueCafeImageToken` → `createImageUploadToken(contentType, 'cafe-items')` → 200 `{ success, message, data: { clientToken, expiresAt, maxSizeBytes, allowedContentTypes, upload: { method, url, headers } } }`.
- Disallowed methods: `router.all` answers 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow` header (`GET /items/:itemId` rejects `DELETE` with 405; items are never hard-deleted).

---

## 3. File Index

### `src/` — root

| Path            | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Exports       |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------- |
| `src/app.js`    | Express app factory: disables `x-powered-by`, sets `trust proxy` from `env.trustProxy`, mounts helmet, cors, `clientType`, pino-http, `express.json({ limit: '1mb' })`, `express.urlencoded`, routes, notFound, errorHandler. CORS options: `*` in dev/test (when `env.corsOrigins === '*'`), otherwise an origin callback that allows requests with NO `Origin` header (native mobile, curl, server-to-server) and origins listed in `env.corsOrigins`; an unlisted origin gets no CORS headers (browser blocks it, no error response). Methods GET/POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE/OPTIONS, allowed headers `Content-Type` + `Authorization`, `credentials: false` (no cookies), preflight `maxAge` 600s. pino-http skips auto-logging for `/health*` and `customProps` adds `clientType` to every request log | `createApp()` |
| `src/server.js` | Entry point: connects Prisma, starts HTTP server on `env.PORT`, graceful shutdown on SIGTERM/SIGINT/unhandledRejection/uncaughtException (10s force-exit timeout)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | —             |

Never prints the password                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `prisma/seed-owner.js`                                                                | Testable owner-seed logic (no I/O besides repository calls). `seedOwner(source)`: validates the 4 `SEED_OWNER_*` vars with the signup rules (`emailField`, `fullNameField`, `phoneField`, `PASSWORD_REGEX` + max 72; issue messages never echo values) → throws `createAppError(400, …, 'SEED_CONFIG_INVALID')` listing every bad/missing var; `findByEmail` → existing OWNER = `ALREADY_EXISTS`, existing non-OWNER = `EXISTS_WITH_OTHER_ROLE` (nothing changed either way: no password overwrite, no role change); otherwise `createUser` with `crypto.randomUUID()`, role OWNER, status ACTIVE, `mustChangePassword` false, gender `STAFF_DEFAULT_GENDER`, `hashPassword` hash. No audit row written (decision: default none). Exports `seedOwner`, `describeSeedResult(result)`, `SeedResult` (CREATED/ALREADY_EXISTS/EXISTS_WITH_OTHER_ROLE), `SEED_CONFIG_INVALID`                                            |

### `src/config/`

| Path                      | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Exports                        |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------ |
| `src/config/env.js`       | Loads dotenv, validates env vars via Zod (exits on failure with a per-variable issue list), validates SMTP vars in non-test mode, accepts optional `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN` (no default, not required; server-only, never returned to clients), validates `CORS_ORIGINS` (bare-origin format; wildcard/empty rejected when `NODE_ENV=production`), validates `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` (min 32, placeholder values rejected) and `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN` (number + s/m/h/d), accepts `REFRESH_SESSION_CLEANUP_GRACE_DAYS`, `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, freezes result with derived `refreshRateLimitMax` (var, else 60, else 100000 in test), `isDev`, `isProd`, `isTest`, `corsOrigins` (`'*'` or a trimmed, lowercased array), `trustProxy`, and exposes `schema` | `env` (object, default export) |
| `src/config/logger.js`    | Pino logger (pino-pretty in dev, silent in test); redacts `req.headers.authorization`, `req.headers.cookie`, `password`, `*.password`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `logger` (default export)      |

### `src/errors/`

| Path                     | Purpose                                                                                                                                          | Exports                                                                              |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `src/errors/AppError.js` | Factory for operational errors with `isOperational: true`; accepts an `AppErrors` definition or a raw status code (code defaults to `APP_ERROR`) | `createAppError(statusCodeOrDef, message?, code?, details?)`, re-exports `AppErrors` |

### `src/middlewares/`

| Path                                             | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Exports                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/middlewares/errorHandler.middleware.js`     | Global 4-arg error handler. Every body is `{ success: false, message, code, errors?, details? }`: malformed JSON→400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`, ZodError→400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `errors[{field,message}]`, Prisma P2002→409 (`USER_ALREADY_EXISTS`), Postgres RESTRICT violation (`isRestrictViolationError`)→409 `RESOURCE_IN_USE`, operational AppError→own status, its own `code`, and forwards optional `details` object (e.g. `sessionCount` on `CLASS_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS`), else→500 generic with `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR`; logs 5xx/non-operational. The rate limiter (429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`) and `notFound` (404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`) reach it through `createAppError`, so they carry their code too.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `errorHandler` (default)                                                                                                                                        |
| `src/middlewares/notFound.middleware.js`         | Forwards 404 AppError (`ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`) for unmatched routes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `notFound` (default)                                                                                                                                            |
| `src/middlewares/loadPlan.middleware.js`         | `loadPlan({ rejectArchived = false })` factory (run after `authenticate` + `authorize`): loads the plan named by `req.params.id` with `findById` onto `req.plan`; unknown or malformed id → 404 `PLAN_NOT_FOUND` (the id column is TEXT, so a malformed id is just not found and the id format is not revealed); `rejectArchived` → 409 `plan_archived`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `loadPlan`                                                                                                                                                      |
| `src/middlewares/loadClass.middleware.js`        | `loadClass()` middleware (runs after `authenticate` + `authorize`): loads class named by `req.params.id` via `findById` onto `req.class`; unknown or malformed id → 404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `loadClass`                                                                                                                                                     |
| `src/middlewares/methodNotAllowed.middleware.js` | `methodNotAllowed(allowedMethods)`: sets the `Allow` header and passes 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` to the error handler. Used with `router.all` after the real plan routes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `methodNotAllowed`                                                                                                                                              |
| `src/middlewares/validate.middleware.js`         | Validation factories (each forwards the ZodError on failure): `validateBody(schema)` replaces `req.body` with the parsed data (unknowns stripped unless the schema is strict); `validateParams(schema)` replaces `req.params`; `validateQuery(schema)` stores the parsed query on `req.validatedQuery` (Express 5 `req.query` is read-only); `validateByRole(validatorsByRole)` runs the validator of `req.user.role` (after `authenticate`) and answers 403 `FORBIDDEN` for a role without an entry                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `validateBody`, `validateParams`, `validateQuery`, `validateByRole`                                                                                             |
| `src/middlewares/authenticate.middleware.js`     | Bearer-JWT guard. Reads `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (scheme case-insensitive; missing/malformed → 401 `UNAUTHORIZED` with `NO_TOKEN_PROVIDED` message), `verifyAccessToken` (`TokenExpiredError` → 401 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`, other `JsonWebTokenError` → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, non-jwt errors rethrown), loads user by `sub` via `findAuthById` (missing `sub` or user not found → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, so existence is not revealed), `assertCanLogin` for status (PENDING → 403 `REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE`, DISABLED → 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`), sets `req.user = { id, role, status }` from the DB (token role ignored). All errors via `next(err)`. No mustChangePassword check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `authenticate`                                                                                                                                                  |
| `src/middlewares/authorize.middleware.js`        | `authorize(...allowedRoles)` factory: throws `Error("authorize: unknown role …")` at registration if an argument is not a `Role` value; at request time no `req.user` → 401 `UNAUTHORIZED`; `req.user.role` not in allowedRoles → logs one `logger.warn` (`AUTHORIZATION_DENIED`: userId, role, method, URL without query, ipAddress, clientType, ISO timestamp) and returns 403 `FORBIDDEN`; else `next()`. Must run after `authenticate`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `authorize(...roles)`                                                                                                                                           |
| `src/middlewares/clientType.middleware.js`       | Sets `req.clientType` (`web` \| `mobile` \| `unknown`) from the `user-agent` and `origin` headers via `detectClientType`, using `env.corsOrigins` as the allow-list; always calls `next()`. Logging/analytics only: used by pino-http `customProps`, NOT stored in the DB (no sessions/audit columns), NOT a security signal                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `clientType` (default)                                                                                                                                          |
| `src/middlewares/rateLimiter.middleware.js`      | express-rate-limit factory using `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`/`RATE_LIMIT_MAX` (defaults 15 min / **1000**; in test env the max defaults to 100000 unless `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` is set in `process.env`; an `options.max` / `options.windowMs` overrides both); standard `RateLimit-*` headers on, legacy `X-RateLimit-*` off; over-limit → `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` 429 via `next(err)`. Store is in-memory (per process, per IP). `authLimiter` is one shared instance (one per-IP counter across all auth routes using it) Plan limiters (SL-MP-02/03): `createPlanLimiter(options?)` keys by `${req.user.id}_${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip)}` (IP only when unauthenticated); `planLimiter` (POST /plans) and `planReadLimiter` (GET /plans) are two separate instances. Class limiters (SL-CLS-02): `classLimiter` (POST/PATCH/PUT/deactivate) and `classReadLimiter` (GET /classes) instances created via `createPlanLimiter`, placed after `authorize` so 403 does not consume budget. Cafe limiters (SLAPP-79/80): `cafeLimiter` (cafe writes and the image token) and `cafeReadLimiter` (cafe reads), two more `createPlanLimiter()` instances with no custom options, so they use the same defaults as every other limiter (`RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS` / `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, default max 1000; 100000 in test). All export from the module. | `createAuthLimiter`, `authLimiter`, `createPlanLimiter`, `planLimiter`, `planReadLimiter`, `classLimiter`, `classReadLimiter`, `cafeLimiter`, `cafeReadLimiter` |

| `src/utils/jwt.util.js`             | Access-token JWTs: HS256 only, secret `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET`, expiry `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`; payload `{ sub: userId, email, role, type: 'access' }` (+ `iat`, `exp`); `email` was added 2026-10-11 and is omitted when the caller passes none. Verify pins `algorithms: ['HS256']` and rejects `type !== 'access'`; throws jsonwebtoken `JsonWebTokenError` / `TokenExpiredError` (callers map to 401)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `signAccessToken({ userId, email, role })` → string, `verifyAccessToken(token)` → payload                                          |
| `src/utils/token.util.js`           | Opaque refresh tokens: 32 random bytes as base64url (43 chars); SHA-256 hex hash (64 chars) for DB storage/lookup                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `generateRefreshToken()`, `hashToken(token)`                                                                                       |
| `src/utils/requestMeta.util.js`     | Request metadata for audit/session rows. `ipAddress` = `req.ip`, `userAgent` = `user-agent` header (undefined if absent). Shape matches `createAuditLog` `meta`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `getRequestMeta(req)` → `{ ipAddress, userAgent }`                                                                                 |
| `src/utils/clientType.util.js`      | Pure client classifier. `detectClientType({ userAgent, origin, allowedOrigins })`, in order: (1) `Origin` present (trimmed, lowercased) and in `allowedOrigins` (or `allowedOrigins === '*'`) → `web`; (2) `Origin` present but not allowed → `unknown` (even with an OkHttp UA, so a foreign/spoofed origin is never trusted); (3) no Origin + UA word match `okhttp`, `retrofit`, `ktor`, `dalvik`, `apache-httpclient` (Kotlin/Android stacks) → `mobile`; (4) no Origin + UA starting `Mozilla/` (browsers, same-origin/navigation) and not containing `node`/`undici`/`node-fetch` → `web`; (5) otherwise (curl, Postman, Node/Next.js server-side fetch, empty/non-string UA) → `unknown`. Both headers are spoofable outside a browser: never use for auth | `ClientType` (`WEB`, `MOBILE`, `UNKNOWN`), `detectClientType`                                                                      |
| `src/utils/html.util.js`            | HTML escaping for user-supplied text placed in HTML (email bodies). `escapeHtml(value)`: converts the value to a string and replaces `& < > " '` with entities (single regex pass, so `&` is never double-escaped). Used for `fullName` in `notify.service` html bodies                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `escapeHtml(value)`                                                                                                                |
| `src/utils/otp.util.js`             | OTP generation. `generateOtp()`: `crypto.randomInt(0, 10**OTP_LENGTH)` (CSPRNG) turned into a string and zero-padded to `OTP_LENGTH` = 4 digits (e.g. `"0042"`). The OTP is never logged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `generateOtp()` → 4-digit string, `OTP_LENGTH` (4)                                                                                 |

| `src/utils/pick.util.js`            | `pick(source, keys)`: copies only the listed keys the object owns (`Object.hasOwn`, so inherited keys never match)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `pick`                                                                                                                             |
| `src/utils/sort.util.js`            | `parseSort(raw, allowedFields, fallback)` turns `price` / `-price` into `{ field, direction: 'asc'                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | 'desc' }`; returns `null` for a field that is not allowed (the validator reports it). | `src/routes/health.routes.js` | `GET /health` (liveness: `{ status, uptime, timestamp }`) and `GET /health/ready` (DB readiness via `SELECT 1`: 200 `{ status: 'ready', db: 'up' }` or 503 `{ status: 'not_ready', db: 'down' }`); plain JSON, not the `success/message/data` envelope                                                               | `router`           |

| `src/routes/auth.routes.js`                     | Auth routes: `GET /me` (`authenticate` → `me`, any active role, no limiter/body), `POST /logout` (shares `refreshLimiter` with refresh, i.e. the same stricter counter → `authenticate` → `validateBody(refreshTokenSchema)` → `logout`; Bearer access token required, checked before the body), `POST /refresh` (own limiter instance `refreshLimiter` = `createAuthLimiter({ max: env.refreshRateLimitMax, windowMs: env.REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS })`, default 60 per 15 min per IP, separate counter from `authLimiter` → `validateBody(refreshTokenSchema)` → `refresh`), `POST /reset-password` (own `createAuthLimiter()` instance `resetPasswordLimiter` → `validateBody(resetPasswordSchema)` → `resetPassword`), `POST /verify-otp` (own `createAuthLimiter()` instance `verifyOtpLimiter` → `validateBody(verifyOtpSchema)` → `verifyOtp`), `POST /forgot-password` (own `createAuthLimiter()` instance `forgotPasswordLimiter`, separate counter from `authLimiter` → `validateBody(forgotPasswordSchema)` → `forgotPassword`; default limits: `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` 1000 per 15 min per IP, in-memory), `POST /register` (`authLimiter` → `validateBody(registerSchema)` → `register`; JSON only, optional `profileImageUrl`), `POST /register/complete`, `POST /login` (all `authLimiter` + `validateBody(schema)`; login uses `loginSchema`) | `src/controllers/auth.controller.js`            | Auth HTTP handlers (thin). `me`: calls `getProfile(req.user.id)`, 200 `PROFILE_FETCHED_SUCCESS` with `data: { user }`; errors → `next(err)`. `logout`: calls the service's `logout(req.body.refreshToken, req.user, getRequestMeta(req))` (imported as `logoutSession`), always 200 `LOGOUT_SUCCESS` with `data: null` for an authenticated caller; errors → `next(err)`. `refresh`: calls `refreshTokens(req.body.refreshToken, getRequestMeta(req))`, 200 `TOKEN_REFRESH_SUCCESS` with `data: { tokens }` (same token fields as login); errors → `next(err)`. `resetPassword`: calls the service's `resetPassword({ email, resetToken, newPassword, meta })`, 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_SUCCESS` with `data: null`. `verifyOtp`: calls `verifyResetOtp({ email, otp, meta })`, 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED_SUCCESS` with `data: { resetToken, expiresAt }`. `forgotPassword`: calls `requestPasswordReset({ email: req.body.email, meta: getRequestMeta(req) })`, always 200 `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_REQUESTED` with `data: null` (identical for every email). `register`: calls `registerMember(req.body)`, 201 `REGISTER_SUCCESS` with the public user. `completeRegister`: calls `completeMemberRegistration(req.body)`, 200 `REGISTRATION_COMPLETE_SUCCESS`. `login`: builds meta `{ ipAddress, userAgent }` via `getRequestMeta` (imported from `utils/requestMeta.util.js`), calls `loginWithPassword(req.body, meta)`, responds 200 `Messages.LOGIN_SUCCESS` with `data: { user, tokens }` via `sendSuccess`; errors → `next(err)`                                                                                     |
| `src/services/auth.service.js`                  | Business logic: `getProfile(userId)` (`findPublicById` → `toPublicUser`; missing user → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`), `registerMember(input)` (order: `findByEmail` → 409; `hashPassword`; `createUser` with `profileImageUrl ?? null` — the URL was already validated by `registerSchema`; no upload/delete happens here; creates PENDING MEMBER with a `crypto.randomUUID()` id), `completeMemberRegistration` (`findById` → `runGuards(registrationGuards)` → `completeRegistrationTx` → `toCompletedRegistration`), `loginWithPassword({ email, password }, meta)`: `findForLogin` → bcrypt compare against `user?.passwordHash ?? lazy dummy hash` (dummy = hash of a random UUID, created once with the real cost factor) → on unknown email/wrong password `recordFailureSafely` (reason INVALID_CREDENTIALS, userId only if user exists) + 401 `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` → only then `assertCanLogin` (on 403 records ACCOUNT_PENDING for PENDING, ACCOUNT_DISABLED for anything else, rethrows) → `startLoginSession` (refresh token + SHA-256 hash, `expiresAt = now + REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`, `createLoginSessionTx`, `signAccessToken`) → returns `{ user: toPublicUser(user), tokens: { accessToken, accessTokenExpiresIn, refreshToken, refreshTokenExpiresAt } }`. `recordFailureSafely` swallows audit-write errors and logs only `{ reason, userId, errorName, errorCode }` with `Messages.LOGIN_AUDIT_WRITE_FAILED` (never the error message, password, hash or tokens), so the HTTP response never changes. `refreshTokens(refreshToken, meta)` (refresh ticket; exposed as `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh` since part 2b): `findUsableSession` → `findByTokenHash(hashToken(token))`; unknown → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; **revoked → reuse**: `rejectReusedToken` = `revokeAllByUser(userId)` then 401 `INVALID_TOKEN` (checked before expiry, no `replacedBy` column); expired → 401 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`. Then `loadUserForRefresh` → `findAuthWithEmailById` (missing → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; `assertCanLogin` → 403 for PENDING/DISABLED/unknown status, sessions are not revoked in that case). Then new `generateRefreshToken`, `expiresAt = now + REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`, `rotate(session.id, { userId, tokenHash: hashToken(new), expiresAt, meta }, now)`; `null` (another request used the token first) → plain 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`, NO revoke-all (changed in part 2b, so a double-click does not log the user out everywhere; a revoked token presented later is still reuse). Returns the same shape as login `tokens` (`accessToken` signed with the DB email + role, `accessTokenExpiresIn`, `refreshToken`, `refreshTokenExpiresAt`). No audit row, no `lastLoginAt` update, DB errors propagate (500). `logout(refreshToken, user, meta)` (user = `req.user`): `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx({ tokenHash: hashToken(token), userId: user.id, meta })`; the session is revoked and one `LOGOUT` audit row written (same transaction) only if the token belongs to the authenticated user and is still active; unknown, foreign, already revoked tokens do nothing and write nothing, and the response is identical (idempotent, no information leak, no revoke-all); a DISABLED/PENDING user cannot call it (`authenticate` → 403); DB errors propagate (500). Does not reuse `startLoginSession` (it writes login side effects)                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `registerMember(input)`, `completeMemberRegistration(input)`, `loginWithPassword(input, meta)`, `refreshTokens(refreshToken, meta)`, `logout(refreshToken, user, meta)`                                          |
| `src/services/auth.guards.js`                   | Sync guards chain (Chain of Responsibility) for registration completion | `src/validators/auth.validation.js`             | Zod schemas. `registerSchema.profileImageUrl` uses `trustedImageUrl.optional()` imported from `uploads.validation` (shared; same rules as before). Internal helpers: `phoneField`, `optionalIntField`, `emailField` (shared by register + login + staff: trim, lowercase, max 254, email format), `fullNameField` (trim, 2–100). Every error text comes from `Messages` (no inline literals; the password policy text still comes from `PASSWORD_POLICY_MESSAGE` in `password.util.js`). registerSchema (fullNameField, emailField, E.164-style phones, optional `whatsappNumber`, `gender`, optional `birthDay`/`birthMonth` (both or neither, day checked against the month's max, Feb 29 allowed), optional `profileImageUrl` — ""/null/blank → absent, trimmed string ≤ 2048 chars that must pass `isTrustedImageUrl`, error `IMAGE_URL_INVALID` — password policy regex, max 72), completeRegistrationSchema (`registrationId` UUID, `emergencyContact` with required phone and optional name/relationship, `waiverAccepted` must be literal `true`), `refreshTokenSchema` (also used as-is by logout; `{ refreshToken }`: string, trimmed, min 1, missing/blank/non-string → `REFRESH_TOKEN_REQUIRED`; unknown keys stripped; no format check on purpose, an unknown token is a 401 not a 400), forgotPasswordSchema (`{ email: emailField }`), verifyOtpSchema (`email: emailField`; `otp`: string, trimmed, must match `^\d{OTP_LENGTH}$` (4 digits, built from `OTP_LENGTH` in `otp.util`) — missing/non-string → `OTP_REQUIRED`, wrong format incl. non-ASCII digits → `OTP_FORMAT_INVALID`, leading zeros kept), resetPasswordSchema (`email: emailField`; `resetToken`: string, trimmed, min 1 → `RESET_TOKEN_REQUIRED`; `newPassword`: shared `passwordField` = string, max 72, `PASSWORD_REGEX` with `PASSWORD_POLICY_MESSAGE`, missing → `PASSWORD_REQUIRED`); `passwordField` is also used by registerSchema; all three new schemas strip unknown keys; loginSchema (emailField; password string 1–72 with `Messages.PASSWORD_REQUIRED` / `PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH`, **no** policy regex so rules don't leak and older passwords still work)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `{ registerSchema, completeRegistrationSchema, loginSchema, refreshTokenSchema, forgotPasswordSchema, verifyOtpSchema, resetPasswordSchema, fullNameField, emailField, phoneField }`                             |
| `src/services/password-reset.service.js`        | Password-reset business logic. `requestPasswordReset({ email, meta })` (email already normalized by `emailField`): `findByEmail` → `getSkipReason(user)` (private: no user → `UNKNOWN_EMAIL`; ACTIVE → null; PENDING → `ACCOUNT_PENDING`; any other status → `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, deny by default). Skipped: one `createAuditLog(prisma, { action: PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED, reason, email, meta })` (userId NULL, also for existing PENDING/DISABLED users), no OTP, no email, resolves `undefined`. ACTIVE: `generateOtp` → `hashPassword(otp)` (bcrypt) → `expiresAt = now + OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES` → `createOtpTx` (invalidates old OTPs, stores the new one, writes the `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` audit row, all in one tx) → `sendOtpInBackground` (private): `sendPasswordResetOtp(...)` is NOT awaited; a rejection is caught and logged as `{ userId, errorName, errorCode }` with `PASSWORD_RESET_EMAIL_FAILED` (never the OTP, the email address or the error message). Resolves `undefined` for known and unknown emails. A DB error in lookup/audit/`createOtpTx` propagates (500). No cooldown (not in the ticket). `verifyResetOtp({ email, otp, meta })`: `findLatestByEmail` → a row whose user is missing or not ACTIVE is replaced by `null` → `runGuards(verifyOtpGuards, { otp, now, maxAttempts: env.OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS })` (null → `OTP_INVALID`, so unknown email / PENDING / DISABLED look exactly like a wrong OTP; used, already verified, attempts reached, invalidated, expired → their own errors, with NO bcrypt compare, no attempt count and no audit) → `comparePassword(otp, row.otpHash)`. Wrong → private `failWrongOtp(row, meta)`: `incrementAttempts` (atomic) → if `attempts >= OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` also `invalidateOtp` and error = `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED` (429), else `OTP_INVALID` (400) → `createAuditLog(prisma, { userId, action: PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_FAILED, reason: <error code>, email, meta })` (the submitted OTP is never stored) → throws. Correct → `generateRefreshToken()` as `resetToken` → `markVerifiedTx({ otpId, userId, email, resetTokenHash: hashToken(resetToken), now, meta })` (token hash + `OTP_VERIFIED` audit in one transaction) → returns `{ resetToken, expiresAt: row.expiresAt }` (the raw token only exists in this return value; neither it nor the OTP is logged). Response time differs between unknown email (guard throws) and wrong OTP (bcrypt compare), accepted by the user. `resetPassword({ email, resetToken, newPassword, meta })`: same lookup (private `findLatestOtpOfActiveUser(email)`, shared with `verifyResetOtp`: missing or non-ACTIVE user → `null` → `OTP_INVALID`) → `runGuards(resetPasswordGuards, { otp, now, resetTokenHash: hashToken(resetToken) })` → `hashPassword(newPassword)` → `resetPasswordTx` (password + `mustChangePassword = false`, active `RefreshSession`s revoked, OTP `usedAt`, `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED` audit, one transaction); returns nothing; nothing sensitive logged or audited. Known gap: already issued access JWTs stay valid until they expire (`JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`) because `authenticate` does not check sessions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `{ requestPasswordReset, verifyResetOtp, resetPassword }`                                                                                                                                                        |
| `src/services/otp.guards.js`                    | Chain of Responsibility for password-reset OTPs (same style as `auth.guards`: sync guards taking a context `{ otp, now, maxAttempts, resetTokenHash }`, throw `createAppError(AppErrors.X)` or return; run with `runGuards` imported from `auth.guards`). `otp` = latest `PasswordResetOtp` row or null. Guards: `ensureOtpExists` (null → `OTP_INVALID`, same as a wrong OTP so unknown emails are not revealed), `ensureNotUsed` (`usedAt` → `OTP_ALREADY_USED`), `ensureNotVerified` (`verifiedAt` → `OTP_ALREADY_USED`; verify chain only, so an OTP cannot mint a second reset token), `ensureAttemptsLeft` (`attempts >= maxAttempts` → `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED` 429), `ensureNotInvalidated` (`invalidatedAt` → `OTP_INVALID`), `ensureNotExpired` (`expiresAt <= now` → `OTP_EXPIRED`), `ensureVerified` (no `verifiedAt` → `OTP_INVALID`), `ensureResetTokenMatches` (`otp.resetTokenHash !== resetTokenHash` → `OTP_INVALID`; both are SHA-256 hashes). Chains: `verifyOtpGuards` = exists → not used → not verified → attempts left → not invalidated → not expired; `resetPasswordGuards` = exists → not used → verified → not invalidated → not expired → token matches. `ensureAttemptsLeft` runs before `ensureNotInvalidated` so an OTP closed for too many attempts reports `OTP_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED`. The async bcrypt OTP comparison and the account-status check (only ACTIVE users) are NOT guards: the service does them. Used by `password-reset.service.js:23` (`verifyOtpGuards` and `resetPasswordGuards`, run with `runGuards`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `ensureOtpExists`, `ensureNotUsed`, `ensureNotVerified`, `ensureAttemptsLeft`, `ensureNotInvalidated`, `ensureNotExpired`, `ensureVerified`, `ensureResetTokenMatches`, `verifyOtpGuards`, `resetPasswordGuards` |
| `src/services/login.policies.js`                | Strategy pattern for login by account status. Private `Map` status → policy: ACTIVE → allow (no throw), PENDING → throws `AppErrors.REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE` (403), DISABLED → throws `AppErrors.ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (403); any other/missing status → `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (deny by default, never allow). Uses a `Map` so inherited keys (`constructor`, `__proto__`) cannot match a policy. To add a status: add one map entry. Called by `loginWithPassword` after the password check and by `authenticate`
