                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `assertCanLogin(user)` (map is not exported)                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `src/repositories/session.repository.js`        | Refresh-session DB operations (all take/return HASHES only; hashing is done by callers with `token.util.hashToken`). `findByTokenHash(tokenHash)` → `{ id, userId, expiresAt, revokedAt }` or null (does NOT check revoked/expired: the caller must). `revokeById(id, now = new Date())` → `updateMany` where `revokedAt` is null; returns `true` if it revoked the session, `false` if already revoked/unknown (never overwrites an earlier `revokedAt`). `revokeAllByUser(userId, now = new Date(), client = prisma)` → revokes all active sessions of the user, returns the count (`client` may be a transaction client; `setCoachStatus` revokes inside its transaction). `rotate(oldSessionId, { userId, tokenHash, expiresAt, meta? }, now = new Date())`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — conditional `updateMany({ id, revokedAt: null })` revokes the old session; if 0 rows matched it returns `null` (nothing written, so of two parallel refreshes with the same token exactly one wins), otherwise creates the new session inline (app UUID, hash only, ip/UA from `meta`, NULL when absent; no `lastLoginAt`, no audit row) and returns `{ sessionId }`; a failing insert (e.g. duplicate hash, P2002) rolls the revoke back. `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx({ tokenHash, userId, now = new Date(), meta = {} })`: ONE `$transaction` (options `ROTATE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS`) — `updateMany where { tokenHash, userId, revokedAt: null }` sets `revokedAt` (so an unknown token, another user's token and an already revoked token all match 0 rows); 0 rows → returns `false`, nothing else written, no audit row; otherwise `tx.user.findUniqueOrThrow` reads the email, `createAuditLog(tx, { userId, action: LOGOUT, email, meta })`, returns `true` (an audit failure rolls the revoke back). Used by `logout` in `auth.service.js`. `deleteStaleSessions(graceMs, now = new Date())`: `deleteMany` where `expiresAt < now - graceMs` OR `revokedAt < now - graceMs` (a null `revokedAt` never matches), returns the deleted count, throws `RangeError` if `graceMs` is not > 0 (reuse detection needs revoked rows to stay for a while); NOT scoped to a user; no caller yet (runner pending). `rotate`'s `$transaction` uses `ROTATE_TRANSACTION_OPTIONS` (`maxWait` and `timeout` 10 s each, constant in the file) because Prisma's 2 s / 5 s defaults made parallel rotations fail on the remote pooler; the other transactions keep the defaults. Does not check expiry, user status or reuse detection (service job). `findByTokenHash` is called by `refreshTokens` and `logout`, `rotate` and `revokeAllByUser` (reuse detection) by `refreshTokens`, (all in `auth.service.js`); `revokeById` has no caller since logout moved to `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx` (kept and tested); `deleteStaleSessions` has no caller (verified by grep: no code, no npm script). Login part (unchanged): `createLoginSessionTx({ userId, tokenHash, expiresAt, now, meta? })`: ONE interactive `prisma.$transaction` — (a) `user.update` sets `lastLoginAt = now` and reads back the user's email, (b) creates `RefreshSession` (stores the token HASH only; `meta.ipAddress`, `meta.userAgent`), (c) creates the `LOGIN_SUCCESS` AuditLog row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)`. All-or-nothing (a missing user → Prisma P2025, duplicate hash → P2002, both roll back everything). Returns `{ sessionId }`. `meta` is `{ ipAddress?, userAgent? }`; omitted fields are stored as NULL. Called by `loginWithPassword` (`startLoginSession`) | `createLoginSessionTx(input)`, `findByTokenHash`, `revokeById`, `revokeAllByUser`, `rotate`, `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx`, `deleteStaleSessions`                                                             |
| `src/repositories/password-reset.repository.js` | DB access for `PasswordResetOtp` (only hashes stored; callers pass `otpHash` = bcrypt of the OTP and `resetTokenHash` = `hashToken(token)`). `client` = prisma client or a transaction client. `createOtp(client, { userId, otpHash, expiresAt, ipAddress })` → row (app UUID). `invalidateOpenOtps(client, userId, now)` → `updateMany` sets `invalidatedAt` on rows with `usedAt` and `invalidatedAt` null. `findLatestByUserId(userId)` → newest row or null. `findLatestByEmail(email)` → newest row of the user with that exact email, plus `user: { id, email, status }`, or null (callers pass the normalized lowercase email). `incrementAttempts(id)` → atomic `increment`, returns `{ attempts }`. `invalidateOtp(id, now)` → sets `invalidatedAt`. `markVerifiedTx({ otpId, userId, email, resetTokenHash, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — sets `verifiedAt` + `resetTokenHash` and writes the `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED` audit row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)` (an audit failure rolls both back); returns nothing. `createOtpTx({ userId, email, otpHash, expiresAt, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — invalidate open OTPs, create the new OTP (`meta.ipAddress`), write `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` audit row via `createAuditLog(tx, …)`; returns `{ otpId }`. `resetPasswordTx({ otpId, userId, email, passwordHash, now, meta? })`: ONE `prisma.$transaction` — FIRST `passwordResetOtp.updateMany({ id, usedAt: null })` sets `usedAt` and throws `OTP_ALREADY_USED` if 0 rows matched (the loser of a race with the same token fails before changing anything), then update `User.passwordHash` + `mustChangePassword = false`, `refreshSession.updateMany` sets `revokedAt = now` where `revokedAt` is null, write `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED` audit row; returns nothing. It does not check the OTP/token (that is the service's job). No expiry/attempt logic here                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `createOtp`, `invalidateOpenOtps`, `findLatestByUserId`, `findLatestByEmail`, `incrementAttempts`, `invalidateOtp`, `markVerifiedTx`, `createOtpTx`, `resetPasswordTx`                                           |

| Path                                  | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Exports                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/repositories/user.repository.js` | Prisma calls only: `findByEmail(email)` (single lookup by email; the old email-OR-phone lookup is gone because phone is no longer unique), `findById` (selects id, phone, status), `findAuthById(id)` (selects only id, role, status; used by `authenticate`), `findPublicById(id)` (selects exactly the `toPublicUser` fields, never passwordHash; used by `getProfile`), `findAuthWithEmailById(id)` (selects id, email, role, status; used by `refreshTokens`; added because `findAuthById` must keep its exact select), `createUser(data)` (passes `data` straight to Prisma, so `profileImageUrl` is accepted), `findForLogin(email)` (exact-match, case-sensitive lookup — callers pass the normalized lowercase email; selects id, fullName, email, phone, whatsappNumber, gender, birthDay, birthMonth, profileImageUrl, role, status, createdAt **and passwordHash** — must be mapped with `toPublicUser` before leaving the service; returns `null` if not found; used by `loginWithPassword`), `updateProfileImage(id, profileImageUrl)` (`prisma.user.update` of `profileImageUrl` only, selects the public fields without passwordHash; Prisma P2025 → `AppErrors.USER_NOT_FOUND` 404; used by `uploads.service.js`), `updateStatusIfCurrent(id, { from, to }, client = prisma)` (`updateMany` where the user has status `from`; returns 0 or 1, so a PENDING user is left alone; used by `setCoachStatus`), `completeRegistrationTx` (PENDING→ACTIVE + EmergencyContact in one tx; maps P2002/count 0 → REGISTRATION_ALREADY_COMPLETED) | `findByEmail`, `findById`, `findAuthById`, `findAuthWithEmailById`, `createUser`, `findForLogin`, `updateProfileImage`, `updateStatusIfCurrent`, `completeRegistrationTx` |
| `src/repositories/user.mapper.js`     | Whitelist safe public fields (toPublicUser: id, fullName, email, phone, whatsappNumber, gender, birthDay, birthMonth, `profileImageUrl` (defaults to null when absent/undefined), role, status, createdAt — never passwordHash) and completion fields (toCompletedRegistration: id, status, registrationCompletedAt)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `toPublicUser(user)`, `toCompletedRegistration(user)`                                                                                                                     |

| `src/controllers/staff.controller.js`  | Thin handlers. `createStaff`: `actor = { id, role }` from `req.user`, `getRequestMeta(req)`, calls service `createStaff(actor, req.body, meta)`, 201 `STAFF_CREATED_SUCCESS` with `data: { ...user, emailSent }`. `createCoach`: same actor and meta, calls `createCoach`, 201 `COACH_CREATED_SUCCESS` with `data: { ...fullCoachView, emailSent }`. `tempPassword` is deliberately dropped in both (never in response/logs); errors → `next(err)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `createStaff`, `createCoach`                                  |
| `src/services/staff.service.js`        | Staff business logic. Private `registerStaffUser(actor, payload, requestMeta, { duplicateError, createProfile })`: role policy via `canCreate` (denied → `STAFF_CREATE_DENIED` audit with `Messages.STAFF_CREATE_DENIED_REASON(actor.role, targetRole)` + 403), duplicate email check (`findByEmail`, any account) → `duplicateError`, UUID, CSPRNG temp password and BCrypt hash, ONE interactive `prisma.$transaction`: `createStaffUser`, the optional `createProfile(tx, user)` hook, `STAFF_CREATED` audit; P2002 race → `duplicateError`; post-commit `sendStaffInvitation` (errors caught and logged without secrets). `createStaff(actor, payload, meta)` = wrapper (409 `USER_ALREADY_EXISTS`), returns `{ user: toStaffResponse(user), tempPassword, emailSent }`. `createCoach(actor, payload, meta)`: splits `coachType`, `bio`, `status` (default ACTIVE) from the user fields, forces role COACH, user status from `UserStatusByCoachStatus` (INACTIVE → DISABLED), `createProfile` = `createCoachProfile` inside the same transaction, duplicate → 409 `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS`, returns `{ coach: toCoachResponse(user, profile), emailSent }` | `{ createStaff, createCoach }`                                |
| `src/repositories/staff.repository.js` | Staff DB operations: `createStaffUser(data, tx)` creates the user with `status: data.status ?? ACTIVE` (coach creation passes DISABLED for an INACTIVE coach), `mustChangePassword: true`, `gender: STAFF_DEFAULT_GENDER`, and role and other fields from data (`profileImageUrl` passes through); supports running inside an interactive Prisma transaction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `{ createStaffUser }`                                         |

| `src/services/staff.policies.js`       | Strategy pattern: `Map<callerRole, Set<targetRole>>` deciding allowed staff role creation. MEMBER → MEMBER; COACH → COACH (own role only); ADMIN → ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER; OWNER → OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, MEMBER; any other caller role is denied                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `canCreate(callerRole, targetRole)`                           |

### Coach feature (`src/controllers`, `services`, `repositories`, `validators`, `routes`)

| Path                                   | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Exports                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/routes/coach.routes.js`           | Mounted at `/coaches`. Every route starts with `authenticate` (bad token → 401, pending/disabled → 403). `GET /` → `authorize(...COACH_READER_ROLES)` → `validateListCoachesQuery` → `listCoaches`. `GET /me` → `authorize(COACH)` → `getMyCoach` (declared before `/:coachId`). `GET /:coachId` → reader roles → `validateCoachIdParam` (UUID) → `getCoach`. `PATCH /:coachId` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER, COACH)` → `validateCoachIdParam` → `validateUpdateCoach(UPDATE_FIELDS_BY_ROLE)` → `createUpdateCoachHandler(UPDATE_FIELDS_BY_ROLE)`. `PATCH /:coachId/status` → `authorize(ADMIN, OWNER)` → `validateCoachIdParam` → `validateCoachStatusBody` → `setCoachStatus`. No DELETE route (no hard delete), no rate limiter                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `router` (default export)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `src/controllers/coach.controller.js`  | Thin handlers, actor = `{ id, role }` from `req.user`. `listCoaches` (uses `req.validatedQuery`) → 200 `{ success, data: [...], pagination }` (no message); `getCoach`, `getMyCoach` → 200 `{ success, data }`; `createUpdateCoachHandler(fieldsByRole)` returns the PATCH handler and picks the allowed fields by `req.user.role` (no entry → `[]`) → 200 `COACH_UPDATED_SUCCESS`; `setCoachStatus` → 200 `COACH_STATUS_UPDATED_SUCCESS`; errors → `next(err)`. Coach creation is in `staff.controller`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `listCoaches`, `getCoach`, `getMyCoach`, `createUpdateCoachHandler`, `setCoachStatus`                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `src/services/coach.service.js`        | `listCoaches({ caller, query })`: `getListAccess(caller.role)` decides filters, search fields and view, then repository list + `buildPagination`. `getCoach({ caller, coachId })`: 404 if missing, `assertCanSee`, `selectCoachView`. `getMyCoach(caller)`: `findCoachByUserId`, 404 `COACH_NOT_FOUND` without a profile, full view. `updateCoach({ actor, coachId, changes, allowedFields, meta })`: load (404) → `assertCanEdit` → `pick(changes, editableFields(role, allowedFields))` → email check (`findByEmail`, other account → 409 `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS`; own email is fine) → ONE transaction: `updateCoachWithUser` (user columns and coach columns) + `COACH_UPDATED` audit (reason = field NAMES only, never values) → full view; P2002 → 409, P2025 → 404. `setCoachStatus({ actor, coachId, status, meta })`: `assertManager`, load (404), ONE transaction: `updateCoachStatus` (only if the status differs, else nothing else is written), `updateStatusIfCurrent` (user follows per `UserStatusTransitionByCoachStatus`, PENDING untouched), on INACTIVE `revokeAllByUser(userId, now, tx)`, `COACH_STATUS_CHANGED` audit (reason `OLD->NEW`); returns the fresh full view | `listCoaches`, `getCoach`, `getMyCoach`, `updateCoach`, `setCoachStatus`                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `src/services/coach.access.js`         | Every role-dependent coach rule in one place. `isCoachManager(role)`; `getListAccess(role)`: managers → full view, filters `status` and `coachType`, search fields fullName + email; everyone else → public view, status forced to ACTIVE, no `coachType` filter, search fields fullName only; `selectCoachView(caller, coach)` (full for managers and the owner of the profile, else public); `assertCanSee` (a coach the caller may not see → 404 `COACH_NOT_FOUND`, never 403); `assertCanEdit` (role without edit fields → 403; managers pass; a coach edits only their own profile: another visible coach → 403, an invisible one → 404); `assertManager` (403); `editableFields(role, requested)` (caps the route's list by `UPDATE_FIELDS_BY_ROLE`); `toFullView`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `isCoachManager`, `getListAccess`, `selectCoachView`, `toFullView`, `assertCanSee`, `assertCanEdit`, `assertManager`, `editableFields`                                                                                                                                              |
| `src/repositories/coach.repository.js` | One shared select: coach columns + user `{ id, fullName, email, phone, profileImageUrl }` (never passwordHash). `createCoachProfile(data, client)`; `findCoachById(id)`; `findCoachByUserId(userId)`; `updateCoachWithUser(id, { userFields, coachFields }, client)` = ONE nested Prisma write (`coach.update` with `user: { update }`), always stamps `updatedAt` explicitly because Prisma only bumps it when a coach column is written; `updateCoachStatus(id, status, client)` = `updateMany` where status differs, true when changed; `listCoaches({ status, coachType, search, searchFields, skip, take })` = `$transaction([findMany, count])` with the same `where`, order `createdAt desc, id asc`; the search is a case-insensitive `contains` on the listed user columns with `\`, `%` and `_` escaped (Prisma does not escape LIKE wildcards)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `createCoachProfile`, `findCoachById`, `findCoachByUserId`, `updateCoachWithUser`, `updateCoachStatus`, `listCoaches`                                                                                                                                                               |
| `src/repositories/coach.mapper.js`     | Two whitelist views. `toCoachResponse(user, coach)` = full: `id` (coach id), `userId`, `fullName`, `email`, `phone`, `profileImageUrl`, `bio`, `coachType`, `status` (the coach's), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`. `toPublicCoachResponse(user, coach)` = public: exactly `id`, `fullName`, `profileImageUrl`, `bio`. Never password hash, role or user status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `toCoachResponse`, `toPublicCoachResponse`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `src/validators/coach.validation.js`   | `createCoachSchema` (`.strict()`: staff fields incl. required phone, required `coachType`, optional `bio` (trimmed, max 1000), optional `profileImageUrl` (`trustedImageUrl`), optional `status`). `buildUpdateCoachSchema(allowedFields)`: only those fields, all optional, `.strict()`, at least one key (`COACH_UPDATE_EMPTY`); `bio` and `profileImageUrl` accept `null` to clear them, `phone` does not; `status` is never part of it. `coachStatusBodySchema` (`{ status }` only). `listCoachesQuerySchema` (managers: `page` ≥ 1 default 1, `limit` 1–100 default 20, optional `search` trimmed max 100 (blank = none), `status`, `coachType`, `.strict()`) and `publicListCoachesQuerySchema` (same without `status`/`coachType`, which are rejected with 400, not ignored). `coachIdParamSchema` (UUID). Middlewares: `validateCreateCoach`, `validateListCoachesQuery` and `validateUpdateCoach(fieldsByRole)` (both via `validateByRole`, one schema per role), `validateCoachIdParam`, `validateCoachStatusBody` SL-MP-03 refactor: `page` and `limit` now come from the shared `pageField` / `buildSizeField` (same messages, defaults and limits).                                   | `createCoachSchema`, `listCoachesQuerySchema`, `publicListCoachesQuerySchema`, `coachIdParamSchema`, `buildUpdateCoachSchema`, `coachStatusBodySchema`, `validateCreateCoach`, `validateListCoachesQuery`, `validateCoachIdParam`, `validateUpdateCoach`, `validateCoachStatusBody` |

### Membership Plans feature (`src/constants`, `src/validators`, `src/repositories`, `prisma/seeds`)

| Path                                            | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Exports                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `src/validators/membershipPlan.validation.js`   | Zod schema `membershipPlanSchema` for membership plan entities/rows. Cross-field rules: `creditType === 'TIME'` requires `creditsIncluded === 0` and `creditRefill === null`; non-time plans require `creditsIncluded >= 1`; `creditRefill === 'MONTHLY'` allowed only with `billingCycle === 'MONTH'`; recurring cycles require `kind === 'SUBSCRIPTION'`; trims strings and coerces price string/number to decimal string. Extended for SLAPP 84/106: validates `term` (optional string max 100), `recurring` (boolean, default true), `periodMonths` (optional int 1/3/6/12), `joiningFee` (number >= 0, default 0). `validateCreatePlan`: strips UI helper fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc`; enforces mutual exclusivity between `membershipId` (UUID) and `membership: { name, description }` (both together -> 422). SL-MP-03: `listPlansQuerySchema` and `validateListPlansQuery`. SL-MP-04/05 & SLAPP 84/106: `validateUpdatePlanBody` (rejects non-editable fields incl. `joiningFee`, allows `membershipId` UUID or null to reassign/detach, rejects nested `membership` object) and `validatePlanStatusBody`. | `{ membershipPlanSchema }`                                                     |
| `src/validators/pagination.validation.js`       | Shared query fields: `pageField` (coerced int 1..`MAX_PAGE`, default 1, `PAGE_INVALID`), `buildSizeField({ message, max, defaultValue })` for `limit` (coach) and `pageSize` (plans)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `pageField`, `buildSizeField`                                                  |
| `src/repositories/membershipPlan.mapper.js`     | `toMembershipPlanResponse(plan)`: converts DB model to application shape. Derives virtual `section`: `'online_coaching'` when `creditType === 'TIME'`, else `'class_membership'`. Normalizes Decimal `price` and `joiningFee` to JavaScript Numbers. Extended for SLAPP 84/106: maps `membershipId` (UUID or null), nested `membership` `{ id, name, description }` (null for standalone plans), `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`, `joiningFee`, `creditSummary`, `creditNote` (alias for creditSummary), and `activeClientCount` (interim stub returning 0). Exports `toSection(creditType)` and `toEditableFields(source)` (includes `membershipId`, `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `{ toMembershipPlanResponse, derivePlanSection }`                              |
| `src/repositories/membershipPlan.repository.js` | Minimal Prisma repository for `membershipPlan`: `upsertByExternalRef`, `findById`, `findByExternalRef`, `findByName`, `updatePlan`. Extended for SLAPP 84/106: all plan reads (`findById`, `findByExternalRef`, `findPlans`, `findActiveByName`) include `{ membership: true }`. `createPlanWithAudit` supports Mode 2 atomic transaction: creates `Membership` entity, `MembershipPlan` entity, and `AuditLog` row in ONE Prisma `$transaction`. Test cleanup helper `deleteTestPlans` in `tests/helpers/membershipPlan.helper.js`. SL-MP-03: `buildListWhere`, `listPlans` (`listByColumn` / `listByNameOrder`), `countNonArchivedByCreditType`. SL-MP-04/05: `findActiveByName` with `excludeId`, `countActiveClients(planId)` placeholder seam, `updatePlanWithAudit`, `changePlanStatusWithAudit`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `{ upsertByExternalRef, findById, findByExternalRef, findByName, updatePlan }` |
| `src/services/plan.service.js`                  | `createPlan` (SL-MP-02, extended SLAPP 84/106): 3 creation modes: Mode 1 (existing `membershipId` verified via `membership.repository.findById`, else 422 `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND`), Mode 2 (new `membership: { name, description }` created atomically in ONE transaction with plan + audit row; duplicate name -> 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`), Mode 3 (standalone with `membershipId: null`). Helper fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc` stripped and never persisted. `listPlans({ query })` (SL-MP-03). SL-MP-04/05 & SLAPP 84/106: `getPlan({ plan })` (full shape); `updatePlan({ actor, plan, next, meta })` (accepts `membershipId` UUID or null, verifies membership exists -> 422 if not; rejects `joiningFee` 422 `PLAN_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE`; audit diff includes `changes.membershipId`); `changePlanStatus({ actor, plan, status, reason, meta })` (changes only status; never touches memberships or client assignments).                                                                                                                                                                                           | `createPlan`, `listPlans`, `toSectionCounts`, `isPlanNameUniqueError`          |
| `src/controllers/plan.controller.js`            | Thin handlers: `createPlan` (201 + `Location`), `listPlans` (200 `{ success, data, meta }`, reads `req.validatedQuery`) SL-MP-04/05: `getPlan`, `updatePlan` (200 + `PLAN_UPDATED_SUCCESS`), `changePlanStatus` (200 + `PLAN_STATUS_UPDATED_SUCCESS`); they read `req.plan` and `req.validatedBody`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `createPlan`, `listPlans`                                                      |
| `src/routes/plan.routes.js`                     | `GET /` → `authenticate`, `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)`, `planReadLimiter`, `validateListPlansQuery`, `listPlans`. `POST /` → `authenticate`, `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)`, `planLimiter`, `validateCreatePlan`, `createPlan`. Mounted at `/api/v1/plans` SL-MP-04/05 added: `GET /:id` (OWNER, ADMIN, COACH, `planReadLimiter`, `loadPlan()`), `PATCH /:id` (OWNER, ADMIN, `planLimiter`, `loadPlan({ rejectArchived: true })`, `validateUpdatePlanBody`), `PATCH /:id/status` (OWNER, ADMIN, `planLimiter`, `loadPlan()`, `validatePlanStatusBody`), then `router.all` 405 handlers for `/:id` (Allow GET, PATCH) and `/:id/status` (Allow PATCH).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | router                                                                         |

Detail views include `scheduleSummary`, `recurringSchedule`, and `upcomingInstancesSummary`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `toClassResponse`                                                                                                                |

**Client upload flow (Kotlin/Retrofit/OkHttp):** (1) `POST /api/v1/uploads/profile-image/token` with JSON `{ "contentType": "image/png" }` → `data.upload` (`method`, `url`, `headers`) + `data.expiresAt`; (2) the client sends `PUT` to `data.upload.url` with exactly `data.upload.headers` and the raw image bytes as the body (OkHttp `RequestBody` of the same mimetype; at most `data.maxSizeBytes`, before `expiresAt`) straight to Vercel Blob; the JSON response of that PUT contains `url`; (3) `POST /api/v1/auth/register` as plain JSON including `"profileImageUrl": "<that url>"`. The upload request format (URL, header names, API version 12) was taken from the installed `@vercel/blob` 2.8.0 SDK source and has NOT been exercised against the real API (**Unverified** against the live service).

### `tests/`

Integration tests hit the real DB from `.env` (remote Neon, ~300ms/query, ~1.6s per interactive transaction) via supertest + `createApp()`, or call repositories directly. DB-backed suites set `jest.setTimeout(30000)` in-file (jest's 5s default is too short) and clean up their own users/audit rows. There is no empty placeholder test file any more (`auth.me.test.js` was filled in the Ticket 6 work). ## 4. Prisma Models & Enums

**Enums:** `Role` (MEMBER, COACH, ADMIN, OWNER) · `AccountStatus` (PENDING, ACTIVE, DISABLED) · `Gender` (MALE, FEMALE, NON_BINARY, PREFER_NOT_TO_SAY) · `Relationship` (PARENT, SPOUSE, SIBLING, CHILD, FRIEND, GUARDIAN, OTHER) · `AuditAction` (LOGIN_SUCCESS, LOGIN_FAILED, STAFF_CREATED, STAFF_CREATE_DENIED, PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED, PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED, PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_FAILED, PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED, LOGOUT, COACH_UPDATED, COACH_STATUS_CHANGED) · `CoachType` (SALARIED, FREELANCE) · `CoachStatus` (ACTIVE, INACTIVE) · `LoginFailureReason` (INVALID_CREDENTIALS, ACCOUNT_PENDING, ACCOUNT_DISABLED — still in the schema but no longer a column type: `AuditLog.reason` is now plain text) · `PlanKind` (SUBSCRIPTION, CREDIT_PACK, TRIAL) · `PlanBillingCycle` (MONTH, QUARTER, HALF_YEAR, YEAR, ONE_OFF) · `PlanCreditType` (CREDITS, TIME) · `PlanCreditRefill` (MONTHLY, ONCE) · `PlanValidityUnit` (DAY, MONTH) · `PlanStartsOn` (PURCHASE_DATE, FIRST_VISIT) · `PlanVisibility` (PUBLIC, HIDDEN) · `PlanStatus` (ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ARCHIVED) · `CafeItemAvailability` (AVAILABLE, SOLD_OUT) · `CafeItemVisibility` (PUBLIC, STAFF_ONLY)

Generator `prisma-client-js`; datasource `postgresql` with `url = env("DATABASE_URL")`. `DIRECT_URL` is **not** used anywhere in the schema or code.

**Model `User`** — used by: `user.repository`, `staff.repository`, `session.repository` (`lastLoginAt`), `prisma/seed-owner.js` (via `createUser`)

| Field                     | Type               | Constraints                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `id`                      | String             | `@id` (UUID set by app, no DB default)                                         |
| `fullName`                | String             | —                                                                              |
| `email`                   | String             | `@unique`                                                                      |
| `phone`                   | String             | not unique (unique index dropped)                                              |
| `whatsappNumber`          | String?            | optional                                                                       |
| `gender`                  | Gender             | —                                                                              |
| `birthDay`                | Int?               | optional                                                                       |
| `birthMonth`              | Int?               | optional                                                                       |
| `profileImageUrl`         | String?            | optional; Vercel Blob URL                                                      |
| `passwordHash`            | String             | —                                                                              |
| `role`                    | Role               | `@default(MEMBER)`                                                             |
| `status`                  | AccountStatus      | `@default(PENDING)`                                                            |
| `mustChangePassword`      | Boolean            | `@default(false)`; true for staff-created users                                |
| `waiverAcceptedAt`        | DateTime?          | optional                                                                       |
| `registrationCompletedAt` | DateTime?          | optional                                                                       |
| `lastLoginAt`             | DateTime?          | set on successful login                                                        |
| `emergencyContact`        | EmergencyContact?  | 1:1 relation                                                                   |
| `coach`                   | Coach?             | 1:1 relation (set for COACH users created through `POST /admin/staff/coaches`) |
| `refreshSessions`         | RefreshSession[]   | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `auditLogs`               | AuditLog[]         | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `passwordResetOtps`       | PasswordResetOtp[] | 1:n relation                                                                   |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime           | `@default(now())`                                                              |
| `updatedAt`               | DateTime           | `@updatedAt`                                                                   |

**Model `EmergencyContact`** — used by: `user.repository.completeRegistrationTx`

| Field          | Type          | Constraints                                 |
| -------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `id`           | String        | `@id` (UUID set by app)                     |
| `userId`       | String        | `@unique`, FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`) |
| `name`         | String?       | optional                                    |
| `phone`        | String        | —                                           |
| `relationship` | Relationship? | optional                                    |
| `createdAt`    | DateTime      | `@default(now())`                           |
| `updatedAt`    | DateTime      | `@updatedAt`                                |

**Model `Coach`** — coach profile of a login account. Only coach-specific data; name, email, phone and image stay on `User`. Used by: `coach.repository` (and `staff.service.createCoach` through it)

| Field       | Type        | Constraints                                         |
| ----------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`        | String      | `@id` (UUID set by app); the `coachId` of the API   |
| `userId`    | String      | `@unique`, FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`)         |
| `coachType` | CoachType   | `@@index`                                           |
| `bio`       | String?     | optional, max 1000 enforced by validation           |
| `status`    | CoachStatus | `@default(ACTIVE)`, `@@index`                       |
| `createdAt` | DateTime    | `@default(now())`                                   |
| `updatedAt` | DateTime    | `@updatedAt` (also set explicitly on coach updates) |

**Model `RefreshSession`** — opaque refresh tokens; only the SHA-256 hash is stored. Used by: `session.repository.createLoginSessionTx`. The repository now has `findByTokenHash`, `revokeById`, `revokeAllByUser`, `rotate` (refresh ticket part 1/5), `refreshTokens` (service) uses them via `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh`; `logout` (service) revokes sessions through `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx` via `POST /api/v1/auth/logout` (authenticated, scoped to the caller, audited); password reset revokes all active sessions in its own transaction. No `replacedBy` column (decided: not needed; reuse detection = revoked token presented → `revokeAllByUser` + 401, to be built in the service).

| Field                     | Type      | Constraints                                 |
| ------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String    | `@id` (UUID set by app)                     |
| `userId`                  | String    | FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`), `@@index` |
| `tokenHash`               | String    | `@unique`                                   |
| `expiresAt`               | DateTime  | —                                           |
| `revokedAt`               | DateTime? | optional                                    |
| `ipAddress` / `userAgent` | String?   | optional                                    |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime  | `@default(now())`                           |

**Model `PasswordResetOtp`** — one row per password-reset OTP request. Used by: `password-reset.repository.js` and `password-reset.service.js` (forgot-password, verify-otp, reset-password). Only hashes are stored (bcrypt OTP hash, SHA-256 reset-token hash).

| Field            | Type      | Constraints                                                      |
| ---------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`             | String    | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                          |
| `userId`         | String    | FK to User (`onDelete: Cascade`), `@@index([userId, createdAt])` |
| `otpHash`        | String    | bcrypt hash of the 4-digit OTP                                   |
| `expiresAt`      | DateTime  | OTP expiry (now + `OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES`)                          |
| `attempts`       | Int       | `@default(0)`; wrong verifications, capped by `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` |
| `verifiedAt`     | DateTime? | set when the OTP is verified                                     |
| `usedAt`         | DateTime? | set when the password reset completes (no reuse)                 |
| `invalidatedAt`  | DateTime? | set when superseded by a newer OTP or attempts are exhausted     |
| `resetTokenHash` | String?   | `@unique`; SHA-256 of the token returned by verify               |
| `ipAddress`      | String?   | optional                                                         |
| `createdAt`      | DateTime  | `@default(now())`                                                |

**Model `AuditLog`** — login success/failure & staff creation audit trail. Used by: `audit-log.repository` (`createAuditLog`, `recordLoginFailure`), `session.repository`, `staff.service` SL-MP-04/05: nullable `details Json` holds the old and new values of a plan change (`PLAN_UPDATED`: `{ changes: { field: { from, to } }, version: { from, to } }`; `PLAN_STATUS_CHANGED`: `{ from, to, reason }`); `AuditAction` gained `PLAN_UPDATED` and `PLAN_STATUS_CHANGED`. The audit table has no entity-id column, so a plan's entries are found through `reason` = `"<name> (<id>)"`.

| Field                     | Type        | Constraints                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                      | String      | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                                                             |
| `userId`                  | String?     | FK to User (`onDelete: SetNull`); null for unknown email                                                            |
| `action`                  | AuditAction | —                                                                                                                   |
| `reason`                  | String?     | set on failures (LoginFailureReason values or dynamic staff denial reason from Messages.STAFF_CREATE_DENIED_REASON) |
| `email`                   | String?     | attempted email (normalized)                                                                                        |
| `actorId`                 | String?     | caller user id (for staff creation / admin actions)                                                                 |
| `actorRole`               | String?     | caller role (for staff creation / admin actions)                                                                    |
| `ipAddress` / `userAgent` | String?     | optional                                                                                                            |
| `createdAt`               | DateTime    | `@default(now())`; `@@index([userId, createdAt])`                                                                   |

**Model `Membership`** (`@@map("memberships")`) — groups membership plans. Used by: `membership.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.mapper.js`. Raw SQL unique index on `LOWER(name)` (`memberships_name_lower_idx`).

| Field         | Type             | Constraints                                                            |
| ------------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`          | String           | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                |
| `name`        | String           | max 120 chars; unique index on `LOWER(name)`                           |
| `description` | String?          | optional, max 1000 chars                                               |
| `createdAt`   | DateTime         | `@default(now())`                                                      |
| `plans`       | MembershipPlan[] | 1-to-many relation to `MembershipPlan` (onDelete Restrict on the plan) |

**Model `MembershipPlan`** (`@@map("membership_plans")`) — membership plans catalog seeded from Glofox. Used by: `membershipPlan.repository.js`, `membershipPlan.mapper.js`, `prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js`. Note: virtual `section` (`'online_coaching'` when `creditType === 'TIME'`, else `'class_membership'`) is derived at runtime in the mapper/repository and is NEVER stored in the database. Indexes: `status`, `kind`, `creditType`, composite `(status, creditType)` (SL-MP-03), `membershipId` (SLAPP 84/106), unique `externalRef`, partial unique `LOWER(name) WHERE status != 'ARCHIVED'`. The database collation is `C.UTF-8` (byte order, case-sensitive), so name ordering uses `LOWER("name")` in raw SQL (see §16). Note: `activeClientCount` is still a stub seam returning 0 (no assignments table exists yet in the database).

| Field                      | Type              | Constraints                                                                           |
| -------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                       | String            | `@id` (UUID set by app)                                                               |
| `name`                     | String            | max 120 chars; partial unique index on `LOWER(name) WHERE status != 'ARCHIVED'`       |
| `description`              | String?           | optional, max 1000 chars                                                              |
| `membershipId`             | String?           | FK to `Membership` (`onDelete: Restrict`), indexed (`@@index([membershipId])`)        |
| `term`                     | String?           | optional, max 100 chars (e.g. "Monthly", "Annual", "12-Week")                         |
| `recurring`                | Boolean           | `@default(true)`                                                                      |
| `periodMonths`             | Int?              | 1, 3, 6, 12 or null                                                                   |
| `joiningFee`               | Decimal(10, 2)    | `@default(0)`; DB check `>= 0 AND <= 100000`                                          |
| `kind`                     | PlanKind          | SUBSCRIPTION, CREDIT_PACK, TRIAL                                                      |
| `billingCycle`             | PlanBillingCycle  | MONTH, QUARTER, HALF_YEAR, YEAR, ONE_OFF                                              |
| `creditType`               | PlanCreditType    | CREDITS, TIME                                                                         |
| `creditsIncluded`          | Int               | `@default(0)`; DB check `>= 0`; 0 for TIME plans, >= 1 for non-time plans             |
| `creditRefill`             | PlanCreditRefill? | optional; MONTHLY, ONCE; null for TIME plans; MONTHLY only with billingCycle MONTH    |
| `validityValue`            | Int               | DB check `>= 1`                                                                       |
| `validityUnit`             | PlanValidityUnit  | DAY, MONTH                                                                            |
| `price`                    | Decimal(10, 2)    | DB check `>= 0 AND <= 100000`                                                         |
| `currency`                 | String            | `@default("INR")`                                                                     |
| `startsOn`                 | PlanStartsOn      | PURCHASE_DATE, FIRST_VISIT                                                            |
| `oneTimePurchasePerClient` | Boolean           | `@default(false)`                                                                     |
| `visibility`               | PlanVisibility    | `@default(PUBLIC)`; PUBLIC, HIDDEN                                                    |
| `status`                   | PlanStatus        | `@default(ACTIVE)`; ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ARCHIVED                                        |
| `externalRef`              | String?           | `@unique`; Glofox plan code used for idempotent upsert                                |
| `version`                  | Int               | `@default(1)`; bumped automatically via DB trigger ONLY when commercial fields change |
| `createdAt`                | DateTime          | `@default(now())`                                                                     |
| `updatedAt`                | DateTime          | `@updatedAt`                                                                          |

**Models and enums added by SL-CLS-01** (all `@@map` to snake_case plural tables; ids are app-generated UUID strings). Enums `ClassVisibility` (PUBLIC, PRIVATE), `SessionState` (OPEN, FULL, CANCELLED).

- `Class` -> `classes`: `name`, `description?`, `durationMin` (CHECK > 0), `baseCapacity` (CHECK >= 1), `intensity` (CHECK 1-5, NOT NULL; seeded records placeholder 3), `classType` (enum ClassType ONSITE, ONLINE; default ONSITE), `classPassEnabled` (Boolean default false), `visibility`, `classpassSpots` (default 0, CHECK: 0 when classPassEnabled=false, 0..baseCapacity when true), `colour?` (CHECK `#RRGGBB`), `photoUrl?`, `status` (enum ClassStatus ACTIVE, DEACTIVATED, ARCHIVED; default ACTIVE; dropped legacy active column), `externalRef?` (unique), `version` (default 1, bumped by trigger), timestamps. Partial unique index on `LOWER(name) WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'`; index on `status`.
- `ClassCoach` -> `class_coaches` (PK classId + coachId; both FKs CASCADE). `ClassEligiblePlan` -> `class_eligible_plans` (PK classId + planId; class CASCADE, plan RESTRICT).
- `ClassSlot` -> `class_slots`: `classId` (CASCADE), `dayOfWeek` (ISO 1-7), `startTime` (`TIME(0)`, Asia/Dubai wall clock), `capacity?` (override, NULL = base capacity), `active`, timestamps; unique `(classId, dayOfWeek, startTime)`. `ClassSlotCoach` -> `class_slot_coaches` (both FKs CASCADE).
- `ClassSession` -> `class_sessions`: `classId` (RESTRICT), `slotId?` (SET NULL; null = one-off), `startAt`, `endAt` (CHECK endAt > startAt), `capacity` (CHECK >= 1, snapshot), `state` (default OPEN), `isPrivate` (default false), `cancelReason?` (CHECK only when CANCELLED), `overriddenFields` (text[] default []), `version` (trigger), timestamps; unique `(classId, startAt)`; indexes on `startAt`, `slotId`. `ClassSessionCoach` -> `class_session_coaches` (session CASCADE, coach RESTRICT).
- `Coach` gained relations `classes`, `slots`, `sessions`; `MembershipPlan` gained `eligibleClasses` (Prisma-level only).

**Models and enums added by SLAPP-79/80 (Cafe Catalogue)** (mapped to `cafe_categories` and `cafe_items`; ids are app-generated UUID strings). Enums `CafeItemAvailability` (AVAILABLE, SOLD_OUT) and `CafeItemVisibility` (PUBLIC, STAFF_ONLY).

- `CafeCategory` -> `cafe_categories`: `id` (UUID PK), `name` (unique case-insensitive index on `LOWER(name)`), `description?`, timestamps. Has 1:n relation to `CafeItem`.
- `CafeItem` -> `cafe_items`: `id` (UUID PK), `name`, `categoryId` (FK to `cafe_categories`, `onDelete: Restrict`), `description?`, `nutrition?` (free-form JSON object; the API validates only that it is an object with string keys, no fixed fields), `priceAed` (`Decimal(10, 2)`, CHECK > 0), `imageUrl?`, `availability` (`CafeItemAvailability`, default AVAILABLE), `visibility` (`CafeItemVisibility`, default PUBLIC), `isTracked` (Boolean, default true), `stockQuantity?` (Int, CHECK >= 0), `unitCost?` (`Decimal(10, 2)`, CHECK >= 0), `externalRef?` (unique String, Glofox variant ID for idempotent seed), timestamps. Indexes on `categoryId`, `visibility`, `availability`, and `(visibility, availability)`. DB CHECK `cafe_items_tracked_consistency_check` ensures tracked items have non-null stock and unit cost and untracked items have both NULL. There is NO DB constraint tying stock = 0 to `SOLD_OUT`: the rule is service-layer logic in `cafeItem.service` (auto `SOLD_OUT` when tracked stock reaches 0 on create/update; `available` with stock 0 → 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`) and, since SLAPP-79/81 sub-task 2, `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` (quantity and SOLD_OUT in one write), exposed as `PATCH /api/v1/cafe/items/:itemId/stock` since sub-task 3. Restocking never flips `SOLD_OUT` back to `AVAILABLE` automatically. Inventory uses these existing fields without a migration.

## 5. API Endpoints

Auth column: "—" public; "Bearer + role" = `authenticate` then `authorize`. Standard bodies: success `{ success: true, message, data }` (lists add a top-level `pagination`, reads have no `message`); error `{ success: false, message, code, errors? }` (`code` on every error since 2026-10-13). Exception: `GET /api/v1/plans` (SL-MP-03) returns `{ success, data, meta: { page, pageSize, total, counts } }` and takes `pageSize`; the coach list keeps `limit` + `pagination` (see §16, open inconsistency).

| Method           | Path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Auth                              | Rate limit                             | Handler                                                    | Status                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET              | `/health`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | —                                 | —                                      | `health.routes.js`                                         | ✅ done — 200 liveness                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| GET              | `/health/ready`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | —                                 | —                                      | `health.routes.js`                                         | ✅ done — 200 / 503 DB readiness                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ALL              | `*` (unmatched)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | —                                 | —                                      | `notFound.middleware.js`                                   | ✅ done — 404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| POST             | `/api/v1/auth/register`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | —                                 | `authLimiter` (shared)                 | `auth.controller.js`                                       | ✅ done — 201, creates PENDING MEMBER                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| POST             | `/api/v1/auth/register/complete`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | —                                 | `authLimiter` (shared)                 | `auth.controller.js`                                       | ✅ done — 200, PENDING → ACTIVE                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| POST             | `/api/v1/auth/login`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | —                                 | `authLimiter` (shared)                 | `auth.controller.js`                                       | ✅ done — 200, `{ user, tokens }`                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| POST             | `/api/v1/auth/logout`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Bearer (any role)                 | `refreshLimiter` (shared with refresh) | `auth.controller.js` → `auth.service.js` (`logout`)        | ✅ done — 200 `data: null` for any token of an authenticated caller, 401 without a valid access token, writes a `LOGOUT` audit row only when a session was revoked (`auth.logout.test.js`)                                    |
