## 6. Environment Variables

| Name                                 | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `NODE_ENV`                           | `development` \| `test` \| `production`; default `development`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PORT`                               | HTTP port; default `5000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LOG_LEVEL`                          | pino level (`fatal`…`trace`, `silent`); default `info`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CORS_ORIGINS`                       | Comma-separated browser origins or `*`; default `*`. Entries are trimmed, lowercased, empties dropped, and must be bare origins (`http(s)://host[:port]`, no path/query/trailing slash) or startup fails. **In production `*`, unset or an empty list makes the app refuse to start**; `*` stays allowed in development/test. Mobile apps send no `Origin` and are unaffected. `.env.example` documents a placeholder production list (domain not final) |
| `DATABASE_URL`                       | PostgreSQL connection string; required. (`DIRECT_URL` is not read or declared anywhere)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BCRYPT_SALT_ROUNDS`                 | Integer; default `12`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET`                  | **Required**; min 32 chars and must not look like a placeholder (`CHANGE_ME`, `replace_me`, `your_secret`, `random secret`, `placeholder`, `secret_here`, case-insensitive, with `_`, `-` or space between words); the app will not start otherwise. `.env.example` ships `CHANGE_ME_...` on purpose, so a copied template fails until replaced                                                                                                          |
| `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`              | Positive whole number + unit `s`, `m`, `h` or `d` (`/^[1-9]\d*[smhd]$/`); default `15m`. Bare numbers (`900` = 900 ms in jsonwebtoken), `0m`, empty, decimals, uppercase and other units are rejected at startup with a clear error                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`             | Integer; default `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`               | Window in ms; default `900000` (15m)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`                     | Max requests per window; default `1000` (was `10` until 2026-10-10; in test env the limiter uses 100000 unless this is set)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `TRUST_PROXY`                        | `true`/`false`/number/string → Express `trust proxy`; default `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SMTP_HOST`                          | SMTP server hostname (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SMTP_PORT`                          | SMTP server port integer (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SMTP_SECURE`                        | `true`/`false` boolean; default `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SMTP_USER`                          | SMTP auth username (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SMTP_PASS`                          | SMTP auth password (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SMTP_FROM`                          | Sender email address (required when `NODE_ENV` ≠ test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `REFRESH_SESSION_CLEANUP_GRACE_DAYS` | Integer > 0; default `7`. Grace before expired/revoked refresh sessions may be deleted by `deleteStaleSessions` (the runner is not built yet; must stay > 0 for reuse detection)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`       | Window in ms for `/auth/refresh` + `/auth/logout`; default `900000` (15m)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REFRESH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX`             | Optional int > 0; max requests per window per IP for refresh + logout. Unset → 60 (100000 when `NODE_ENV=test`); an explicit value is honoured in every environment. Keep it unset/commented in the `.env` used for tests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES`                 | Integer; default `10`. OTP lifetime for password reset (read by `env.js` since 2026-10-11; also in local `.env`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS`                   | Integer; default `5`. Max wrong OTP attempts per OTP (not in local `.env`, default applies)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`              | Vercel Blob token; optional string, no default (profile image upload token signing; missing → 503 on the token endpoint); server-only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEED_OWNER_EMAIL`                   | **Seed only** (`npm run prisma:seed`), NOT read by `env.js`: owner email, signup email rules (example `owner@example.com`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SEED_OWNER_PASSWORD`                | **Seed only**: owner password, `PASSWORD_REGEX` + max 72, never printed; `.env.example` placeholder is deliberately invalid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SEED_OWNER_FULL_NAME`               | **Seed only**: owner full name, 2–100 chars (example `First Owner`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SEED_OWNER_PHONE`                   | **Seed only**: owner phone, E.164-style (example `+12025550100`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

**Confirmed stale in the local `.env` on 2026-10-11 (names only, read by no code; `.env` not edited, owner asked to remove them):** `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`, `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN`, `JWT_RESET_SECRET`, `JWT_RESET_EXPIRES_IN`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `COOKIE_SECURE`. The local `.env` does not set `RATE_LIMIT_MAX`, `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, `TRUST_PROXY`, `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS` or `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS` (defaults apply).

**Earlier list — present in local `.env` but NOT read by `env.js` (legacy, unused):** `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN` (refresh lifetime is `REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`), `JWT_RESET_SECRET`, `JWT_RESET_EXPIRES_IN`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MINUTES`, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, `COOKIE_SECURE`. (`SMTP_*` moved out of this list: they are read now. Which of the others still exist in the local `.env` is **Unverified**; `OTP_RATE_LIMIT_*` and `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_*` were confirmed present earlier this session.)

## 9. Ticket Progress — Email + Password Login (SLAPP-5)

| #   | Task                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Status  |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
| 1   | Foundation: `User.lastLoginAt`, `RefreshSession`, `AuditLog`, enums, migration `add_login_sessions_audit` (applied, client regenerated); `audit.js`; AppErrors; JWT/refresh env vars (`env.js`, `.env.example`, `.env`)                                                                                                                                                         | ✅ done |
| 2   | Token utilities: `jwt.util.js` (`signAccessToken`, `verifyAccessToken`), `token.util.js` (`generateRefreshToken`, `hashToken`), unit tests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | ✅ done |
| 3   | Validation: shared `emailField` (only SLAPP-3 touch: registerSchema email now uses it, behaviour identical, register tests unchanged), `loginSchema`, new `Messages` (EMAIL_REQUIRED, EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH, EMAIL_INVALID, PASSWORD_REQUIRED, PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH — same text as former literals), unit tests                                                                       | ✅ done |
| 3b  | Cleanup (SLAPP-3/4 files allowed): all inline messages in `auth.validation.js` replaced by `Messages` constants (13 added; register's password field now reuses `PASSWORD_REQUIRED` / `PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH`; no duplicate texts in `messages.js`); verified identical error text for 116 issue messages before/after; Prettier `--write` on 7 SLAPP-3/4 files; no tests changed | ✅ done |
| 4   | Strategy pattern: `login.policies.js` (`assertCanLogin`, status → policy `Map`, deny by default), unit tests; no service/route/repository changes yet                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | ✅ done |
| 5   | Repositories: `findForLogin` (user.repository), `createLoginSessionTx` (session.repository), `recordLoginFailure` + shared `createAuditLog` (audit-log.repository); real-DB integration tests + shared `tests/helpers/testData.helper.js`; no service/controller/route changes                                                                                                  | ✅ done |
| 6   | Service `loginWithPassword`, controller `login`, route `POST /login`, `Messages.LOGIN_AUDIT_WRITE_FAILED`; unit tests (service 13, controller 3) + integration `auth.login.test.js` (18); `auth.service.js` formatted with Prettier                                                                                                                                             | ✅ done |

**SLAPP-5 status: ✅ COMPLETE** (all 6 subtasks + 3b).

**Approved decisions (SLAPP-5):**

- Status check only after password verified: PENDING → 403 `REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE`, DISABLED → 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, ACTIVE → allowed (Strategy-style status policies).
- Unknown email and wrong password → identical 401 `INVALID_CREDENTIALS`; bcrypt compare against a dummy hash when user not found (timing).
- Tokens returned in JSON body: JWT access token (`JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`, 15m); opaque refresh token (`REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS`, 30d), only its SHA-256 hash stored in `RefreshSession`.
- Success: `lastLoginAt`, session and `LOGIN_SUCCESS` audit row written in one transaction. Failures: `LOGIN_FAILED` audit row with reason and attempted (normalized) email; if the audit write fails → log + still return the 401/403.
- Shared `emailField` helper used by `registerSchema` and `loginSchema` (only permitted SLAPP-3 change; behaviour identical).
- `authLimiter` counter shared between register and login.
- Never return `passwordHash`; never log passwords, hashes or tokens.

**Implemented login flow (`POST /api/v1/auth/login`):**

| Step | Case                                                            | Result                                                                                                                                       |
| ---- | --------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1    | Rate limit exceeded (shared `authLimiter`)                      | 429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`                                                                                                                      |
| 2    | Body invalid (`loginSchema`: email format, password 1–72 chars) | 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` + `errors[{field,message}]`; no audit row                                                                            |
| 3    | Unknown email                                                   | 401 `INVALID_CREDENTIALS` (dummy-hash compare); audit `LOGIN_FAILED`/`INVALID_CREDENTIALS`, userId NULL, attempted email                     |
| 4    | Wrong password (any status)                                     | Identical 401 body; audit `LOGIN_FAILED`/`INVALID_CREDENTIALS` with userId                                                                   |
| 5    | Correct password, PENDING                                       | 403 `REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE`; audit reason `ACCOUNT_PENDING`                                                                                |
| 6    | Correct password, DISABLED (or unknown status)                  | 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`; audit reason `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`                                                                                      |
| 7    | Correct password, ACTIVE                                        | 200 `LOGIN_SUCCESS`; one transaction sets `lastLoginAt`, stores the refresh-token hash in `RefreshSession`, writes `LOGIN_SUCCESS` audit row |
| 8    | Unexpected error (e.g. DB down)                                 | 500 generic via `errorHandler`                                                                                                               |

Success body: `{ success: true, message, data: { user: <toPublicUser>, tokens: { accessToken, accessTokenExpiresIn, refreshToken, refreshTokenExpiresAt } } }`. Audit-write failure on steps 3–6 is logged (no secrets) and does not change the response. Refresh rotation is implemented (`POST /api/v1/auth/refresh`, see §10) and logout is implemented (`POST /api/v1/auth/logout`, see §10): `RefreshSession` rows are written at login, read by refresh (`findByTokenHash`) and revoked on rotation, logout (`revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx`), reuse detection and password reset. The `authenticate` middleware (which uses `verifyAccessToken`) now exists and is used by the staff route (see §10).

## 10. Auth, Security & Cross-Cutting Summary

**Auth flow (where each piece lives)**

- Password hashing: `password.util.js` (bcrypt, `BCRYPT_SALT_ROUNDS`, default 12); password policy `PASSWORD_REGEX` (8+ chars with upper, lower, digit; max 72 enforced in schemas).
- Access token: `jwt.util.js` (HS256, `{ sub, email, role, type: 'access' }`, `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`; the email claim is informational, `authenticate` ignores it and re-reads role/status from the DB), issued in `auth.service.startLoginSession`, verified in `authenticate.middleware.js`, which re-reads role/status from the DB (`findAuthById`) and ignores the token's role claim.
- Refresh session: `token.util.js` (opaque token, SHA-256 hash), stored by `session.repository.createLoginSessionTx` in `RefreshSession` (hash only, ip, user agent, `expiresAt`). The repository functions for lookup/revoke/rotate (`session.repository`) are used by `refreshTokens` and `logout` in `auth.service.js`, exposed as `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh` and `/logout`; there is no endpoint to list or revoke sessions of another user.
- Authorization: `authorize.middleware.js` (role allow-list, throws on unknown role, logs every 403 with role/method/URL/IP/clientType/timestamp) + `staff.policies.js` (who may create which role: MEMBER/COACH own role only, ADMIN → ADMIN/COACH/MEMBER, OWNER → all four). The role always comes from the DB user loaded by `authenticate` (token `sub` only); header/query/body roles are ignored (tested). Coach routes add ownership and visibility rules inside `coach.service` (see §12).
- Audit logging: `audit-log.repository.js` (`createAuditLog`, `recordLoginFailure`); actions (9, as in `constants/audit.js` and the schema enum) `LOGIN_SUCCESS`, `LOGIN_FAILED`, `STAFF_CREATED`, `STAFF_CREATE_DENIED`, `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED`, `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_VERIFIED`, `PASSWORD_RESET_OTP_FAILED`, `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED`, `LOGOUT` (written by `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx` in the revoke transaction, only when a session was actually revoked; fields: userId, email, ip, user agent). Login audit writes are best-effort (never change the response); staff audit rows are written inside the creation transaction.
- Staff onboarding: temp password (CSPRNG, 16+ chars) emailed via `notify.service` after commit; user gets `mustChangePassword: true`. There is no change-password endpoint yet and `authenticate` does not enforce the flag.

**Security & cross-cutting**

- helmet defaults, `x-powered-by` disabled, JSON body limit 1 MB, CORS allow-list (see §3 `app.js`, §6 `CORS_ORIGINS`), no cookies/credentials.
- Rate limiting: `rateLimiter.middleware.js` (see §3). Default 1000 requests / 15 min / IP, in-memory store; effective client IP depends on `TRUST_PROXY` (default `false`, so behind a reverse proxy all clients share the proxy IP until it is configured).
- Logging: pino / pino-http (`logger.js`), redacts authorization header, cookie header and password fields; health routes are not auto-logged; `clientType` logged per request; login failures log only reason, userId and error name/code.
- Error handling: `AppError.js` + `errorHandler.middleware.js` (see §3). Validation errors are `400` with `errors[{field,message}]`; unknown errors are generic `500`.
- Client identification: `clientType.util.js` / `clientType.middleware.js` — informational only.

**Known gaps / TODO**

- Not implemented: a runner for `deleteStaleSessions` (the function and `REFRESH_SESSION_CLEANUP_GRACE_DAYS` exist; nothing calls it, so expired/revoked `RefreshSession` rows still accumulate), change-password, `asyncHandler.util.js`, `duration.util.js`, `config/container.js` (all empty).
- Already issued ACCESS tokens stay valid until they expire (default 15 minutes) after logout, reuse detection or a password reset, because `authenticate` checks the user in the DB but not the session (verified live 2026-10-11 and by `auth.logout.test.js`). Accepted decision (no session id in the access token), not a bug.
- A refresh token that was logged out and is later presented to `POST /auth/refresh` counts as reuse: `revokeAllByUser` revokes ALL sessions of that user and the response is 401 (covered by `auth.logout.test.js`). A stale client that refreshes after logging out therefore signs the user's other devices out too.
- Logout requires a valid (not expired) access token: a client whose access token expired must refresh first. There is no "log out of all devices" endpoint.
- `DIRECT_URL` is not configured (Prisma uses `DATABASE_URL` only) — fine unless pooled-connection migrations are needed.
- Migration `20261005201533_add_profile_image_url` exists on disk; applied: `prisma migrate status` on 2026-10-11 reports 9 migrations and "Database schema is up to date".
- Vercel Blob client-upload request format has not been exercised against the real API.
- Legacy unused vars may remain in the local `.env` (see §6).
- No TypeScript leftovers (checked).

**Profile image feature (client uploads to Vercel Blob)** — Task A: `User.profileImageUrl`, optional `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`, `toPublicUser` returns it. Task B/C originally used server-side multer upload; **refactored** to client uploads: `POST /api/v1/uploads/profile-image/token`, `blob.storage.js` = `createImageUploadToken` / `isTrustedImageUrl` / `deleteImage`, multipart/multer removed (`upload.middleware.js` deleted, `multer` uninstalled), `registerSchema` takes optional `profileImageUrl`, `registerMember(input)` stores it as-is; messages `IMAGE_TOO_LARGE` / `IMAGE_UPLOAD_FAILED` / `UPLOAD_REQUEST_INVALID` removed, three token/URL messages added. Task D: DB suite `auth.register.image.test.js` (`@vercel/blob` mocked).

**Refresh flow (`POST /api/v1/auth/refresh`, body `{ refreshToken }`):** `refreshLimiter` (own counter) → `validateBody(refreshTokenSchema)` → `refresh` → `refreshTokens`. Unknown token → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; revoked token presented (reuse) → `revokeAllByUser` + 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; expired → 401 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`; user missing → 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; PENDING/DISABLED → 403; lost race → plain 401 `INVALID_TOKEN` (no revoke-all); success → old session revoked + new one created in one transaction, response `{ success, message: TOKEN_REFRESH_SUCCESS, data: { tokens: { accessToken, accessTokenExpiresIn, refreshToken, refreshTokenExpiresAt } } }`. No audit rows are written for refresh or reuse. The old refresh token stops working immediately; the old ACCESS token stays valid until it expires.

**Deployment note (rate limits):** the login/register limiter (`authLimiter`) is `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` 1000 per `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS` 15 min per IP (the local `.env` sets neither, so defaults apply); refresh/logout are stricter (60 per 15 min per IP). Counters are per IP and in memory (per process). Behind a reverse proxy or load balancer `TRUST_PROXY` must be set to the number of proxy hops (for example `1`), not `true`, otherwise every user shares the proxy's IP (one shared counter) or a client can spoof `X-Forwarded-For`; default `false`.

**Logout flow (`POST /api/v1/auth/logout`, Bearer access token + body `{ refreshToken }`):** `refreshLimiter` → `authenticate` → `validateBody(refreshTokenSchema)` → `logout` → `logout` service → `revokeSessionWithLogoutAuditTx`. No/invalid/expired access token → 401 (checked before the body), DISABLED/PENDING caller → 403, bad body → 400. For an authenticated caller it is always 200 `LOGOUT_SUCCESS` (`data: null`) whether the token was active, foreign, already revoked, expired or unknown. Only a token that belongs to the caller and is still active is revoked, together with exactly one `LOGOUT` audit row (same transaction); nothing else is touched (other devices keep working, no revoke-all). The access token already issued stays valid until it expires. Password reset (`resetPasswordTx`) separately revokes all of the user's active sessions in the same transaction as the password change.

**Client identification + CORS lockdown (2026-10-10):** `clientType.util.js`, `clientType.middleware.js`, `env.js` CORS validation, `app.js` origin allow-list, `.env.example` docs, tests `clientType.util`, `clientType.middleware`, `cors` integration, CORS block in `env.test.js`. No endpoint/DB changes.

**Layer-based restructure (2026-10-10):** `src/modules/*` → `controllers/ services/ repositories/ validators/ routes/`; 22 files moved, ~345 relative `require`/`jest.mock` paths rewritten, no behaviour change.

**Rate limit default (2026-10-10):** `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` default 10 → 1000 in `env.js` and `.env.example` (behaviour change: looser brute-force protection on auth routes); middleware and tests unchanged.

## 12. Coach CRUD — decisions and deviations from the original spec

Decisions: a coach is a login account (Coach profile table linked by `userId`), no duplicate name/email/password/image fields; error shape stays flat with an added `code`; creation goes through the staff route; audit rows reuse `STAFF_CREATED`/`STAFF_CREATE_DENIED` and add `COACH_UPDATED`/`COACH_STATUS_CHANGED` (field names only, never values); out of scope and absent from schema and API: qualifications, specializations, salary, rate, commission, contract dates, notice period, working hours.

| #   | Spec                                                                                                   | Implementation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Note                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | `firstName`, `lastName`, response `fullName`                                                           | `fullName` only (input and output), 2–100 chars                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `User.fullName` is the single name field                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 2   | `POST /coaches`                                                                                        | `POST /api/v1/admin/staff/coaches` (ADMIN/OWNER); no `POST /coaches`                                                                                                                                                                                            | creates the login account; the temp password is emailed (`emailSent` in the response), never returned; generic `POST /admin/staff` now answers 400 for role COACH (breaking for clients that created coaches there) |
| 3   | `phone` optional                                                                                       | `phone` required on create, not clearable on update                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `User.phone` is NOT NULL; normalized to `+digits`                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 4   | `id`                                                                                                   | UUID `id` (the coach id) plus extra `userId` in the full view; `:coachId` must be a UUID                                                                                                                                                                        | malformed id → 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`, unknown id → 404                                                                                                                                                             |
| 5   | error `{ success:false, error:{ code, message } }`                                                     | flat `{ success:false, message, code, errors? }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `errors[{field,message}]` only for validation                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 6   | codes `VALIDATION_ERROR`, `UNAUTHORIZED`, `FORBIDDEN`, `COACH_NOT_FOUND`, `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS` | all five exist; more codes appear: 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`/`TOKEN_EXPIRED` (no token is `UNAUTHORIZED`), 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`/`REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE` for disabled/pending callers, 429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`, 404 `ROUTE_NOT_FOUND`, 500 `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR` | `code` is now on every error response of the API                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 7   | no such endpoint                                                                                       | extra `GET /coaches/me` (COACH only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `/auth/me` does not expose the coach id                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 8   | Coach: view own profile only                                                                           | Coach also lists and reads other ACTIVE coaches in the public view                                                                                                                                                                                              | PRD decision; a COACH sees their own profile in full                                                                                                                                                                |
| 9   | Member: public info only                                                                               | same, ACTIVE coaches only; INACTIVE or unknown = 404                                                                                                                                                                                                            | existence of INACTIVE coaches is never revealed                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 10  | filters `status`, `coachType`, search                                                                  | filters only for ADMIN/OWNER; COACH/MEMBER get 400 if they send them; their search matches `fullName` only; list is forced to ACTIVE                                                                                                                            | email can never be probed by search                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 11  | Coach edits own profile                                                                                | `fullName`, `phone`, `profileImageUrl`, `bio` only; `email`, `coachType`, `status` in the body → 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (not 403); another ACTIVE coach → 403, INACTIVE or unknown → 404                                                                        | 400 is returned before the 403 when the body itself is invalid                                                                                                                                                      |
| 12  | `email` unique                                                                                         | unique across ALL accounts (member, staff, coach), stored lower case; taken by anyone → 409 `COACH_EMAIL_ALREADY_EXISTS`                                                                                                                                        | changing a login email does not revoke sessions; already issued access tokens keep the old `email` claim until they expire (nothing reads it)                                                                       |
| 13  | `profileImageUrl` any URL                                                                              | must be a URL of our blob store (`profile-images/` folder, max 2048); `null` clears it                                                                                                                                                                          | stored on `User`, shared with the account avatar and `PATCH /uploads/profile-image`                                                                                                                                 |
| 14  | `bio`                                                                                                  | max 1000 chars, trimmed, `null` clears it                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 15  | partial update                                                                                         | at least one key (empty body → 400 `COACH_UPDATE_EMPTY`), `.strict()`: unknown keys, `status`, `firstName`, `lastName` → 400                                                                                                                                    |                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 16  | status: activate or deactivate                                                                         | `PATCH /coaches/:id/status` `{ status }`; INACTIVE: user ACTIVE → DISABLED and all refresh sessions revoked; ACTIVE: user DISABLED → ACTIVE; a PENDING user is never touched; repeated calls return 200 and write nothing; no DELETE route                      | reactivating does not bring old sessions back                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 17  | `{ data, pagination }`                                                                                 | same, no `message`; `limit` default 20 max 100, `page` max 100000, newest first; a page past the end → 200 with `data: []` and correct totals; unknown query keys → 400                                                                                         | search is a case-insensitive substring, `%` and `_` are literal, max 100 chars                                                                                                                                      |
| 18  | (nothing about it)                                                                                     | `201` create body is the full view plus `emailSent`; invitation email failure does not fail the request                                                                                                                                                         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Open risks: any authenticated Coach or Member can read names, photos and bios of all ACTIVE coaches (intended public view, email and phone never included); `Coach.status` and `User.status` can diverge if a user is disabled by other means, and reactivating a coach re-enables a DISABLED user; no rate limiter on the coach routes; the substring search cannot use an index (fine at coach-table scale, a `pg_trgm` index is the later fix); interactive transactions use Prisma's default 5 s timeout on the remote DB (same as staff creation); `errorHandler` still has its own P2002 check next to `utils/prismaError.util.js`; the shared dev DB means test suites rely on unique tokens and the 24 h sweep for hygiene.

## 13. Ticket SL-MP-01 — Membership Plan Data Model and Glofox Seed

Implemented ticket SL-MP-01: data model, migration, minimal repository, validator, mapper, seed script, unit and integration tests.

| Area                    | Implementation Details                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Data Model & DB**     | Table `membership_plans`, 8 enums. Audit fields: `id` (app UUID), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`. Commercial versioning: `version Int @default(1)`, bumped by Postgres `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger ONLY when commercial fields change (`price`, `billingCycle`, `creditType`, `creditsIncluded`, `creditRefill`, `validityValue`, `validityUnit`). `externalRef` (`Glofox plan code`) is `@unique` for upsert. Partial unique index on `LOWER(name) WHERE status != 'ARCHIVED'`. Check constraints for price, creditsIncluded, and validityValue. Rollback script at `prisma/migrations/20261024160000_add_membership_plans/down.sql`. |
| **Validation & Mapper** | `membershipPlan.validation.js` with cross-field Zod schema: TIME plans must have 0 credits & null refill; non-time plans must have >= 1 credit; monthly refill only on monthly cycle; recurring cycle only for subscriptions. `membershipPlan.mapper.js` computes virtual `section`: `'online_coaching'` when `creditType === 'TIME'` (7 plans), else `'class_membership'` (53 plans). Decimal prices normalized to numbers.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **Seed Script**         | `prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js` reads `docs/Spirit_Level_Glofox_Business_Data.xlsx` (header row 4) using `exceljs`. Idempotent upsert on `externalRef`. Resolves 2 case-insensitive name collisions by appending parenthetical qualifier from "Membership" column: `Hyrox Peak Membership (Monthly) (Coached)` and `Hyrox Peak Membership (3 months) (Coached)`. Emits derived-field warnings and identifies 10 single-visit candidate plans. Total 60 plans seeded in remote Neon DB. Rerun is 100% idempotent (60 updated, 0 created, 0 skipped).                                                                  |
| **Tests**               | 4 suites, 17 tests: `membershipPlan.mapper.test.js` (4), `membershipPlan.validation.test.js` (8), `membershipPlan.model.test.js` (2), `membershipPlan.seed.test.js` (3). Covers version trigger bump on commercial vs non-commercial edits, case-insensitive partial index constraint, mapper derivations, validation rules, and seed runner idempotency using an xlsx fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## 15. Ticket SL-MP-02 — Create a Membership Plan

Implemented `POST /api/v1/plans`:

- **Security & Authorization:** `authenticate` + `authorize(Role.OWNER, Role.ADMIN)`. `Role.COACH` and `Role.MEMBER` receive `403 FORBIDDEN` and create nothing. Unauthenticated requests receive `401 UNAUTHORIZED`.
- **Rate Limiting:** `planLimiter` protects `POST /api/v1/plans` keyed by authenticated `${req.user.id}_${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip)}` (fallback to IP for unauthenticated) and answers `429 TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` on violation. Active in test environment with configurable limit overrides.
- **Validation (422 Unprocessable Entity):** Custom `validateCreatePlan` middleware returns ALL failing issues together with status 422:
  - Required non-negative `price` (supports coercion from string).
  - `creditType === TIME`: `creditsIncluded` must be 0 or omitted (defaulted to 0), `creditRefill` must be null/omitted.
  - `creditType !== TIME`: `creditsIncluded` required whole number $\ge 1$.
  - Recurring `billingCycle` only if `kind === SUBSCRIPTION` with exact message: `"Only a membership renews monthly."`.
  - `creditRefill === MONTHLY` only allowed when `billingCycle === MONTH`.
  - Enforces `status = 'ACTIVE'` and `version = 1`.
  - `externalRef` stripped silently and not persisted.
- **Name Uniqueness & Race Handling:** Pre-check via `findActiveByName` (case-insensitive check against plans where `status != 'ARCHIVED'`). Concurrency race on `membership_plans_name_lower_active_idx` is caught (`P2002`) and mapped to `409` with code `'duplicate_name'`.
- **Transactional Audit Log:** Atomically creates the `MembershipPlan` row and `AuditLog` row (`action: PLAN_CREATED`, `actorId`, `actorRole`, `reason: ${plan.name} (${plan.id})`, `meta`) inside the same Prisma `$transaction`.
- **Derived Fields & Response Format:** Returns `201 Created` with header `Location: /api/v1/plans/{id}` and body `{ success: true, message, data }`. The `data` payload returns numeric `price`, lowercase string enum values, derived `section` (`'online_coaching'` vs `'class_membership'`), `creditSummary` (e.g. `"15 class credits a month"`, `"1 appointment credit"`, `"Time-based access"`), and `activeClientCount: 0`.
- **Web & Mobile Compatibility:** Verified full compatibility for browser (Next.js with Origin) and native mobile (Kotlin without Origin). Bearer authentication and standard JSON bodies.
- **Documentation:** Complete OpenAPI / Swagger 3.1 specification served at `/api-docs` (Swagger UI; `/api-docs/openapi.yaml` and `/api-docs/openapi.json` for the raw spec; mounted in `app.js` via `docs.routes.js`, not under `/api/v1`) under path `/plans`.

## 16. Ticket SL-MP-03 — List and Search Membership Plans

Implemented `GET /api/v1/plans` (see the header and §3 for files).

- **Access and limits:** `authenticate` + `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN, COACH)`; MEMBER → 403, no token → 401. `planReadLimiter` (own counter, same key as `planLimiter`) → 429 `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`. Order: 401, 403, 429, 422.
- **Query:** `section`, `kind`, `creditType`, `visibility`, `status` (comma list or repeated), `q`, `page`, `pageSize`, `sort`. Enum values are case-insensitive, blank values count as not given. A known param with a bad value → 422 listing every failing field. **Unknown params are stripped (not `.strict()`)** so web and mobile clients can add params; the trade-off (a misspelled name such as `sections=` silently applies no filter) is documented in OpenAPI. Coach and staff schemas stay `.strict()`.
- **Filters:** one `buildListWhere`. Default statuses ACTIVE + INACTIVE; ARCHIVED only when `status` includes it. Section filters on `creditType` (TIME = online_coaching); `section=online_coaching&creditType=class` is an empty 200, not an error. `q` matches name, description and kind label, case-insensitively.
- **Sorting:** `name` (default), `price`, `createdAt`, `-` for descending, `id` as tie-break so paging is stable. The Neon database collation is `C.UTF-8` (case-sensitive: "STRDY" sorts before "Single", "HEROX" before "Hyrox"), so `name` sorts with raw SQL on `LOWER("name")`. The raw query only receives and returns ids (Prisma already filtered), so no WHERE logic is duplicated. A test proves the name path and the column path return the same total and plans for 9 filter sets.
- **`meta.counts`:** `groupBy creditType` on non-archived plans, run in parallel with the list, ignoring `q`, every filter and `status`. Always `{ class_membership, online_coaching }`. Seed: 53 and 7 (plus any non-archived plans created since).
- **Seed facts verified:** 60 plans, all ACTIVE; 7 are TIME (Body Recomposition, HEROX Programme (Female-specific), Hyrox Beginner Program (12 week plan) and (Monthly), Hyrox Personalised Programming, Hyrox Personalised Programming + Nutrition, Nutrition Coaching); `q=nutrition` returns exactly the last two.
- **Performance:** each database round trip is about 200-300 ms from the developer machine to the remote Neon DB, so the endpoint costs: sign-in check (1) + list (1 round trip for `price`/`createdAt`, 2 for `name`). Measured fastest runs for 64 plans: roughly 440-500 ms for `-price` and 700 ms for `name` on that link, i.e. about 100-300 ms of processing beyond the round trips. The list's `findMany` + `count` run in parallel instead of in a `$transaction` (a transaction added about 3 round trips). A hard 300 ms wall-clock assertion against the remote DB is not possible, so the test compares the fastest of 7 runs with 300 ms plus the measured round trip per sequential call. Closer to the database the same code is expected to be well under 300 ms.
- **Open risks:** (1) name ordering cannot use an index and loads every matching plan: fine for a catalogue of tens or hundreds of plans; the later fix is a functional index on `LOWER(name)` (and `pg_trgm` for `q`). (2) `meta.counts` ignoring `status` means the badges include inactive plans. (3) `activeClientCount` is a placeholder (0). (4) The plan list uses `pageSize` + `meta` while the coach list uses `limit` + `pagination`; there is no SL-SV-01 document in the repo, so this inconsistency is unresolved. (5) Validation status codes differ: plans 422, coaches/auth/staff 400.
- **Web and mobile audit (rate limiter):** all endpoints are plain JSON with Bearer auth and `clientType` applies app-wide, so web (Origin) and mobile (no Origin) are both supported everywhere. Endpoints WITHOUT a rate limiter: `GET /auth/me`, `POST /admin/staff`, `POST /admin/staff/coaches`, `GET /coaches`, `GET /coaches/me`, `GET /coaches/:coachId`, `PATCH /coaches/:coachId`, `PATCH /coaches/:coachId/status`, `PATCH /uploads/profile-image`. Public `/health*` are intentionally unlimited.
- **Database change applied:** migration `20261026090000_add_plan_list_index` was applied to the shared Neon dev DB with `prisma migrate deploy`.

## 17. Tickets SL-MP-04 and SL-MP-05 — Get, Update and Change Status of a Membership Plan

Implemented `GET /api/v1/plans/:id`, `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id` and `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id/status` (files in the header and §3).

- **Decisions (approved):** unknown body keys are rejected (422), not stripped, for the two PATCH bodies (the GET list query still strips unknown params); a malformed or unknown `:id` is 404; both PATCH routes share `planLimiter` with `POST /plans`; `plan_in_use` only for a switch between TIME and a non-TIME credit type; inactive plans are editable; the status response keeps the usual envelope with `data: { id, status, updatedAt }`; audit `details` as described in §4; any method other than GET/PATCH on a plan route is 405 (Allow header), also without a token.
- **Versioning check (requested):** the trigger `bump_membership_plan_version` compares exactly `price`, `billingCycle`, `creditType`, `creditsIncluded`, `creditRefill`, `validityValue`, `validityUnit` = `COMMERCIAL_FIELDS` = the SL-MP-01 list (a test reads the function source and also checks that every table column is classified). Writing a field with its current value (also `100` vs `100.00`) does not bump. The app never bumps the version itself.
- **No-op PATCH:** sending only current values returns 200 with the current plan, without a write, version bump or audit row (assumption, not in the ticket).
- **Existing purchases and price changes:** nothing to enforce yet (no purchases table). Contract for the purchase ticket: a purchase must store the plan `version` it was bought on and billing must read that version, so a price change applies from the next billing cycle; purchase must refuse inactive and archived plans (reads `status`).
- **Gaps and assumptions:** (1) no purchases or clients table: `countActiveClients` is a seam returning 0, `plan_in_use` is proven by a unit test with the seam mocked only; (2) an audit entry has no entity id column, so plan entries are matched through `reason`; (3) the status endpoint writes with a compare-and-set, so two concurrent status changes cannot both succeed (the loser gets `invalid_transition`); a PATCH that loses a race with an archive gets `plan_archived`; (4) Postgres cannot drop enum values, so `down.sql` of the audit migration only drops the column.
- **Report only (not changed, as requested): rate limiter and web + mobile audit of the plan endpoints.** Rate limiter: `POST /plans`, `GET /plans`, `GET /plans/:id`, `PATCH /plans/:id`, `PATCH /plans/:id/status` all have one; the 405 routes have none (they do no work). The earlier list of endpoints WITHOUT a limiter in §16 still stands (auth/me, staff creation, coach routes, profile-image PATCH). Web + mobile: all plan endpoints are Bearer-token JSON with the same envelope and error codes, no cookies, no dependence on `Origin`; tests compare a browser-Origin request with a no-Origin request. Gaps found: (a) CORS has no `exposedHeaders`, so browser code cannot read `Allow` (405), `Location` (201 on create) or the `RateLimit-*` / `Retry-After` headers of a 429 from a cross-origin response; mobile is unaffected; fix later in `app.js` (not touched here); (b) `POST /plans` silently strips `externalRef` and forces `status`, while PATCH rejects them with 422 (intentional per the tickets); (c) validation errors are 422 for plans but 400 for coaches/staff/auth.
- **Open risks:** the 405 handlers answer before authentication (they reveal only that the route exists); the plan name check is a read followed by a write, so the unique index and the P2002 mapping are what finally guard a race; `Json` audit details are not indexed.

## 18. Ticket SL-CLS-01 — Class, Slot and Session Data Model (BE-CLS-1, SLAPP-66)

Decisions agreed before coding: table names plural snake_case (`classes`, `class_sessions`, ...; `class_sessions` instead of `session` so it is not confused with `RefreshSession`); version through a DB trigger (`bump_row_version`); `intensity` nullable in the DB with the API ticket (CLS-2) enforcing required 1-5; `externalRef` and slot `capacity` kept; eligible plans left empty; "Hyrox Endurance (90min)" is the canonical Endurance name; the three extra CHECKs (classpass spots, colour, cancel reason) kept. Details of schema, constraints, delete behaviour and seed are in the header and §4.

- **Seed report (Neon dev DB, 2026-10-26):** 13 rows read, 12 classes created, 29 slots created, 0 skipped. Warnings: 1 duplicate programme (Endurance), 1 visibility conflict (On The Move (Private)), 6 coaches not found (George Crewe, Ben Gregory, Luke Skippon, Danielle Crewe, Lloyd Melling, Louie Shilling: no matching coach accounts in the dev DB, so no coach links exist yet; rerun `npm run seed:classes` after the coaches are created).
- **Public classes with zero eligible plans (all 10 public classes):** DEKA Prep, Hyrox Compete, Hyrox Endurance (90min), Hyrox Engine, Hyrox First Lap, Hyrox HIIT, Hyrox Strong (Lower Body), Hyrox Strong (Upper Body), Level X, STRDY x ROXSTARS (Kids Class). The 2 private classes (On The Move (Private), YBV HYROX (Private)) also have none. The booking ticket must decide what "no eligible plans" means before it relies on this table.
- **Open points for later tickets:** DEKA Prep has no slot (not on the timetable that week); the seed never deletes slots or classes that disappear from the sheet; `FULL` is a stored state the booking services must keep in sync (overbooking is allowed per Glofox policy, so no DB check); sessions are generated from slots by a later ticket (slot time is Dubai wall-clock, UTC+4, no DST); the weekly timetable sheet (week of 5 Oct) was used as the standing timetable; compared with "Schedule 2 Weeks" it matches week 1 exactly (29 slots) but week 2 (12-18 Oct, 25 rows) differs: Monday 05:30 Hyrox HIIT exists only in week 2, Saturday 08:00 Endurance has capacity 24 instead of 32, and the 5 Sunday classes (First Lap, Level X, Endurance (90mins), YBV HYROX (Private), STRDY Kids) are absent (possibly a cut-off of the extract), plus 4 coach differences. A product owner must confirm which week is the standing timetable.
- **Test status:** 109 suites / 1687 tests after this ticket; 17 migrations; 14 Prisma models in total (7 new).

## 19. Tickets SL-CLS-02 & SL-CLS-03 — Classes API and Class Eligibility (BE-CLS-2, BE-CLS-3, SLAPP-66)

Implemented 6 endpoints under `/api/v1/classes` and class eligibility management:

- **Security & Authorization:** Every endpoint enforces Bearer auth and RBAC. Rate limiters (`classLimiter` for writes, `classReadLimiter` for reads) are placed AFTER `authorize` so 403s do not consume limiter quota.
- **Visibility & Scoping (Product Decision):**
  - Members see only active public classes; private or inactive classes return 404 `CLASS_NOT_FOUND`.
  - Coaches see all active public classes plus any class where they are assigned as coach (even if private or inactive).
  - Staff (Owner/Admin) see all classes (defaulting to `active=true`, supporting `active=false` and `active=all`).
- **Standardized Response Shape:** Lowercased enums (`public`, `private`), coach objects `[{ id, fullName, profileImageUrl }]`, sorted `eligiblePlanIds`, omitting `externalRef`.
- **Validation Boundaries (422 `VALIDATION_ERROR`):** Name (2-80 trimmed), description (<=1000), durationMin (15-240), baseCapacity (1-200), intensity (1-5), visibility (`public`|`private`), classpassSpots (0..baseCapacity), colour (`#RRGGBB`), photoUrl (https only, max 2048). Validation errors return 422 with all failing fields together via shared `validationError.util.js`.
- **Optimistic Locking:** If `version` is passed in `PATCH /classes/:id`, updates match `{ id, version }`. Stale versions yield 409 `CLASS_VERSION_CONFLICT`. If omitted, last-write-wins.
- **Concurrency & Races:** Case-insensitive active class name pre-checked, and P2002 race mapped to 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`.
- **Deactivation Seam:** Sets `active=false` without deleting sessions. Idempotent 200. Calls `countFutureBookedSessions` interim seam (returns 0 until booking table exists; mocked > 0 in tests to verify 409 `CLASS_HAS_FUTURE_BOOKINGS` with `details: { sessionCount }`).
- **Eligibility Management (`PUT /classes/:id/eligibility`):** Atomic transaction using `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`. Deletes old links, inserts de-duplicated new links. Rejects unknown/archived plans with 422. Public classes require >= 1 plan (empty list = 422); private classes allow empty list (200).
- **Constant Query Batch Loading:** List endpoint uses `CLASS_INCLUDE` (`coaches -> coach -> user`, `eligiblePlans`) + parallel count. Constant query count verified by integration test (1 class vs 12 classes).
- **Web & Mobile Parity:** Verified identical status, headers, and JSON envelopes between browser (Next.js with Origin) and native mobile (Kotlin OkHttp without Origin).
- **RESTRICT Error Handling:** Postgres foreign key RESTRICT violations mapped cleanly to 409 `RESOURCE_IN_USE` via `isRestrictViolationError`.

## 20. Ticket BE-CLS-1 — Class Template CRUD & Lifecycle Management

Implemented in-place on branch `feat/SL-CLS-02-03-classes-api-and-eligibility`:

- **Status Model & Backward Compatibility:**
  - Database schema migrated from boolean `active` to enum `ClassStatus` (`ACTIVE`, `DEACTIVATED`, `ARCHIVED`).
  - To prevent breaking web and mobile consumers, all API responses (`toClassResponse`, `deactivateClass`, `activateClass`, `archiveClass`) continue to expose the computed boolean `active` (`status === 'active'`) alongside canonical `status`.
  - Archived classes return 409 `CLASS_INACTIVE` on `PATCH /:id` and `PUT /:id/eligibility`.
  - Member visibility: Members see only ACTIVE public classes.
  - Staff listing: Excludes ARCHIVED classes by default; `status=all` returns all classes.

- **Required Intensity & Backfill:**
  - Migrated `intensity` column to `NOT NULL` with `CHECK (intensity >= 1 AND intensity <= 5)`.
  - Seeded records backfilled with 3. Flagged in this report and CODEMAP that seeded intensity values are temporary placeholders requiring product owner input.

- **ClassPass Spots Validation:**
  - `classPassEnabled=false` strictly enforces `classpassSpots = 0` (rejects > 0 with 422).
  - `classPassEnabled=true` allows `0..baseCapacity`.
  - DB check constraint `classes_classpass_enabled_check` matches this business rule exactly.

- **OverriddenFields & Seam for Session Editing:**
  - Frozen constants defined in `src/constants/class.js`: `SessionOverriddenField` (`CAPACITY: 'capacity'`, `DURATION: 'duration'`, `COACHES: 'coaches'`, `START_AT: 'startAt'`).
  - Dropped `colour` from session overrides as sessions have no colour.
  - Current status: No code sets `overriddenFields` today; this establishes the precise seam for a future session-edit ticket. Coaches propagation is out of scope.

- **Set-Based SQL UPDATE Session Propagation:**
  - Updating `baseCapacity` or `durationMin` on a class template propagates immediately to future sessions via set-based SQL UPDATE statements:
    - Capacity: `UPDATE class_sessions SET capacity = $baseCapacity WHERE "classId" = $id AND "startAt" > NOW() AND "state" != 'CANCELLED' AND NOT ('capacity' = ANY("overriddenFields"))`
    - Duration: `UPDATE class_sessions SET "endAt" = "startAt" + ($durationMin * interval '1 minute') WHERE "classId" = $id AND "startAt" > NOW() AND "state" != 'CANCELLED' AND NOT ('duration' = ANY("overriddenFields"))`
  - Avoids N+1 per-session loop; verified by integration test across 55 future sessions (past sessions skipped, cancelled skipped, overridden skipped).

- **Activation & Unique Name Conflict Check:**
  - `POST /api/v1/classes/:id/activate` restores deactivated or archived classes to ACTIVE.
  - If another active class has taken the name while deactivated, reactivation is blocked with 409 `CLASS_DUPLICATE_NAME`.

- **Migration & Rollback:**
  - Applied migration `20261026180000_update_class_template_crud` with fully reversible `down.sql`. Tested up and down in scratch schema.

- **Windows Note:**
  - Commands run with `npx.cmd` on developer machines; on Linux use `npx`.

## 21. Tickets BE-SCH-1, BE-SCH-2, and BE-SCH-3 — Scheduling & Session Management

Implemented scheduling, recurring slots, and session management across tickets BE-SCH-1, BE-SCH-2, and BE-SCH-3.

### Implemented Endpoints

- **Slots (BE-SCH-2):**
  - `GET /api/v1/classes/:id/slots` — List recurring slots (`classReadLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin, Coach, Member). Member read follows existing class visibility rules (active + public only; otherwise 403). Exact persisted values returned (`capacity: null` remains null).
  - `POST /api/v1/classes/:id/slots` — Create recurring slots (`classLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin). Accepts canonical body `{ startDate, days: [{ dayOfWeek, times: [{ startTime, endTime, coachIds, capacity }] }] }`. Validates time order, duplicate days/times/coaches, and triggers immediate 21-day rolling generation. Returns saved slots + reconciliation summary.
  - `PATCH /api/v1/classes/:id/slots/:slotId` — Update recurring slot (`classLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin). Updates rule, validates conflicts, and reconciles affected future unbooked non-overridden sessions.
  - `DELETE /api/v1/classes/:id/slots/:slotId` — Soft-deactivate slot (`classLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin). Sets `active = false` and deletes only future, unbooked, non-overridden GENERATED sessions. Past, booked, and overridden sessions are preserved.
- **Sessions (BE-SCH-1 & BE-SCH-3):**
  - `GET /api/v1/sessions?from&to&classId&coachId&state&isPrivate` — Calendar read (`classReadLimiter`, RBAC: all roles). Required `from` and `to` ISO-8601 UTC date bounds (max 92 days). Enforces DB-level predicates, RBAC visibility, and returns calendar-ready instances with live booking/waitlist counts.
  - `POST /api/v1/sessions` — Create one-off session instance from template (BE-SCH-1, `classLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin). Always sets `source: 'one_off'` and `slotId: null`. Inherits durationMin, baseCapacity, and coaches from template if omitted. Returns 201 with `Location: /api/v1/sessions/:id`.
  - `GET /api/v1/sessions/:id` — Get session detail (`classReadLimiter`, RBAC: all roles; Member requires public active class and not private).
  - `PATCH /api/v1/sessions/:id` — Update session occurrence (`classLimiter`). Owner/Admin full edit; Coach can only update `capacity` on own assigned session (otherwise 403). Past sessions are immutable (409 `SESSION_PAST_IMMUTABLE`). Capacity below booked returns 422 `CAPACITY_BELOW_BOOKED`. Mandatory `overrideReasons` enforced for `coachIds`, `startAt`, and `endAt`. Tracks `overriddenFields` and `overrideDetails` JSONB `{ field: { reason, actorId, changedAt } }`.
  - `POST /api/v1/sessions/:id/cancel` — Cancel session (`classLimiter`). Reason required (422 `CANCEL_REASON_REQUIRED`). Coach can only cancel own empty session. Sets `state = 'cancelled'`, calls `getCancelOutcome` adapter, and records cancellation override.
  - `POST /api/v1/sessions/:id/restore` — Restore cancelled session (`classLimiter`, RBAC: Owner, Admin). Restores to `state = 'open'`, re-runs coach and same-startAt conflict checks. Non-cancelled sessions return 409 `SESSION_NOT_CANCELLED`.

### Data Model & Migrations

- Migration: `20261027130000_add_scheduling_fields` applied to Neon DB via non-pooled `DIRECT_URL`.
- `Class`: added `timezone TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Asia/Dubai'` (configurable via env `FACILITY_TIMEZONE`).
- `ClassSlot`: added `startDate DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE` and `endTime TIME(0) NOT NULL`. Dropped unconditional unique constraint; added partial unique index `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX class_slots_active_unique_idx ON class_slots ("classId", "dayOfWeek", "startTime") WHERE active = true`.
- `ClassSession`: added `source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GENERATED'`, `durationMin INTEGER`, `intensity INTEGER`, `classType TEXT`, `facility TEXT`, `description TEXT`, `image TEXT`, and `overrideDetails JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'`.
- Indexes: `class_sessions("startAt", "classId")`, `class_sessions("slotId", "startAt")`, `class_session_coaches("coachId", "sessionId")`.
- Backfill: historical sessions with `slotId != null` backfilled as `GENERATED`, else `ONE_OFF`. Snapshots backfilled from class template. Reversible `down.sql` included.

### Conflict Detection & Concurrency Safety

- Class advisory transaction locks: `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('class_schedule_' || classId))` serializes concurrent scheduling actions per class.
- Conflict checks:
  - Same class + same startAt -> 409 `SESSION_CONFLICT` with `{ conflicts: [{ type, sessionId, startAt, endAt }] }`.
  - Overlapping coach time -> 409 `COACH_CONFLICT` with half-open `[startAt, endAt)` checks across all classes (cancelled sessions free coach time).
  - Slot collision -> 409 `SLOT_CONFLICT`.
  - Version race -> 409 `SESSION_VERSION_CONFLICT`.

### Daily Rolling Generation Job

- Long-running server architecture (`src/server.js`) uses `node-cron` (`src/services/schedule.job.js`).
- Scheduled at 00:05 daily in facility timezone. Batched pre-query optimization prevents N+1 DB round-trips over serverless Neon connection.
- Fully idempotent: `generateSessionsForWindow()` inserts missing occurrences with conflict isolation; one failing class/slot never aborts the rest. Stopped gracefully on SIGINT/SIGTERM.

### Integration Adapters & Seams

- `src/services/schedule.adapters.js` provides integration seam functions:
  - `getBookingStats(sessionId)` -> `{ bookedCount: 0, waitlistCount: 0 }` (wired to reconciliation's booked-skip rule).
  - `getCancelOutcome(sessionId, reason)` -> `{ booking: null, credit: null, waitlist: null }`.
  - `isCoachQualified(coachId, classId)` -> `true`.
  - `isCoachOnLeave(coachId, startAt, endAt)` -> `false`.
- TODO markers and seam contracts defined for upcoming booking and coach leave tickets.

### Web & Mobile Parity & CORS

- CORS `exposedHeaders` updated in `src/app.js`: `Location`, `Allow`, `RateLimit-Limit`, `RateLimit-Remaining`, `RateLimit-Reset`, `Retry-After`.
- Standardized response envelope, lowercase enums, and ISO-8601 UTC dates identical between browser (Origin) and native Kotlin mobile (no Origin).

### Reused vs New Code

- **Reused:** `authenticate`, `authorize`, `classLimiter`, `classReadLimiter`, `loadClass`, `methodNotAllowed`, `sendSuccess`, `sendError`, `sendValidationFailed`, `createAppError`, `AppErrors`, `isUniqueConstraintError`, `isRestrictViolationError`, `prisma` singleton, `class.repository` (`findById`, `validateCoachIds`, `replaceSlotCoaches`), `constants/class.js` (`ClassVisibility`, `SessionState`, `DAY_OF_WEEK`), `app.js` CORS/middleware chain.
- **New:** `src/constants/session.js`, `src/services/schedule.adapters.js`, `src/repositories/schedule.mapper.js`, `src/repositories/schedule.repository.js`, `src/services/schedule.service.js`, `src/validators/schedule.validation.js`, `src/controllers/schedule.controller.js`, `src/routes/session.routes.js`, `src/services/schedule.job.js`.
- **Tests Added:** `tests/unit/schedule.validation.test.js`, `tests/unit/schedule.mapper.test.js`, `tests/unit/schedule.service.test.js`, `tests/unit/schedule.adapters.mock.test.js`, `tests/integration/schedule.slot.test.js`, `tests/integration/schedule.session.test.js`, `tests/integration/schedule.job.test.js`, `tests/integration/schedule.rate-limit.test.js`.
