## 22. SLAPP 84/106 — Memberships API & Plan Creation Modes

Implemented the Memberships API and extended Membership Plans with 3 creation modes, per SLAPP 84 and SLAPP 106.

### Architecture & Fixed Decisions

- **Plan ID & Name:** Plan `id` remains an app-generated UUID; plan `name` remains a separate unique field.
- **Backward Compatibility:** Existing `/api/v1/plans` API contracts, routes, query parameters, status endpoint, sections, counts, field names, and enums are strictly preserved. Response structures are extended with new fields without breaking web (Next.js) or mobile (Kotlin) clients.
- **Client Assignments:** No assignments table exists yet in the database; `activeClientCount` remains an interim stub seam returning 0. Status changes only update plan status and never touch client assignments.

### Memberships API (`/api/v1/memberships`)

- `GET /api/v1/memberships`:
  - RBAC: `OWNER`, `ADMIN`, `COACH`.
  - Rate limiting: `planReadLimiter`.
  - Single Prisma query fetching all memberships with `include: { plans: true }`.
  - Computes `planCount = plans.length` and maps each plan using `toMembershipPlanResponse`.
  - Returns 200 `{ success: true, data: [{ id, name, description, createdAt, planCount, plans }] }`.
- `POST /api/v1/memberships`:
  - RBAC: `OWNER`, `ADMIN`.
  - Rate limiting: `planLimiter`.
  - Input: `{ name, description? }` validated via Zod (`name` trimmed, min 1 max 120; `description` optional max 1000).
  - Uniqueness: case-insensitive duplicate check before write; DB-level race caught via raw SQL unique index on `LOWER(name)` mapping P2002 to 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`.
  - Returns 201 `{ success: true, message: MEMBERSHIP_CREATED_SUCCESS, data: { id, name, description, createdAt } }`.
- Other HTTP methods:
  - `router.all` returns 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` with `Allow: GET, POST`.

### Plan Creation (`POST /api/v1/plans`) — 3 Creation Modes

The plan creation payload supports 3 mutually exclusive modes for membership assignment:

1. **Mode 1 (Existing Membership):** Payload includes `membershipId` (UUID). The service verifies the membership exists via `membership.repository.findById`; returns 422 `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND` if not found.
2. **Mode 2 (New Membership):** Payload includes `membership: { name, description? }`. The service creates the `Membership` entity, the `MembershipPlan` entity, and the `AuditLog` row (`PLAN_CREATED`) in **ONE atomic Prisma `$transaction`**. If membership creation fails (e.g. duplicate name -> 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`) or plan creation fails, the entire transaction rolls back cleanly.
3. **Mode 3 (Standalone):** Neither `membershipId` nor `membership` is provided. The plan is created with `membershipId: null`.

- **Validation Rules:**
  - `membershipId` and `membership` are mutually exclusive; sending both returns 422 `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
  - Helper UI fields `memMode`, `newMemName`, `newMemDesc` are stripped by the validator and never persisted to the database.
  - Plan-name uniqueness, commercial credit validation, and forced `ACTIVE` initial status are strictly preserved.

### Plan Detail, List & Update (`GET`, `PATCH /api/v1/plans`)

- **Responses:**
  - Full plan responses (`GET /api/v1/plans/:id`, `POST /api/v1/plans`, `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id`) include `membershipId`, nested `membership: { id, name, description }` (null for standalone plans), `term`, `recurring`, `periodMonths`, `joiningFee`, `creditSummary`, and `creditNote` (alias for creditSummary).
  - Plan list items (`GET /api/v1/plans`) include `membershipId` and `membership`.
- **PATCH `/api/v1/plans/:id`:**
  - Accepts `membershipId`: valid UUID verifies membership existence (422 `MEMBERSHIP_NOT_FOUND` if missing); `null` reassigns plan to standalone.
  - No new-membership creation via PATCH (sending `membership` object is stripped or rejected).
  - `joiningFee` is explicitly non-editable via API (returns 422 `PLAN_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE`).
  - Audits record `changes.membershipId: { from, to }`.
- **Status Endpoint (`PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id/status`):**
  - Changes only plan status; never alters membership links or client assignments.

### Glofox Seed (`prisma/seeds/membership-plans.seed.js`)

- Re-reads Excel sheet "Memberships" column "Membership".
- Trims values; blank or missing values remain standalone (`membershipId: null`).
- Case-insensitively upserts memberships by name.
- Sets `membershipId` on plans **ONLY if currently null** (`WHERE id = ... AND membershipId IS NULL`), strictly preserving any manual reassignments performed via the API.
- Fully idempotent on repeated runs.

### Data Model & Migration

- Migration `20261027140000_add_memberships_and_plan_fields`:
  - Creates table `memberships` (`id` UUID PRIMARY KEY, `name` TEXT NOT NULL, `description` TEXT, `createdAt` TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()).
  - Creates case-insensitive unique index `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX memberships_name_lower_idx ON memberships (LOWER(name))`.
  - Extends `membership_plans`:
    - `membershipId` UUID NULL REFERENCES `memberships(id)` ON DELETE RESTRICT (indexed via `membership_plans_membershipId_idx`).
    - `term` TEXT NULL.
    - `recurring` BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE.
    - `periodMonths` INTEGER NULL (CHECK `periodMonths IN (1, 3, 6, 12)`).
    - `joiningFee` DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.
  - Backfills existing 60 plans: `term = NULL`, `joiningFee = 0`, `periodMonths` derived from `billingCycle` (`MONTH` -> 1, `QUARTER` -> 3, `HALF_YEAR` -> 6, `YEAR` -> 12, else NULL), `recurring = TRUE` only for `kind = 'SUBSCRIPTION'`.
  - Non-commercial fields: these 5 fields are excluded from version trigger comparisons, so editing them does not bump the plan version.
  - Includes fully reversible `down.sql`.

### Web & Mobile Parity & Rate Limiting

- Bearer-token authentication with JSON envelopes across all endpoints.
- No `Origin`-dependent behavior: tested and verified for browser clients (Next.js with `Origin`) and native mobile clients (Kotlin/Retrofit without `Origin`).
- Rate limiting: `planReadLimiter` on `GET /api/v1/memberships`, `planLimiter` on `POST /api/v1/memberships`, `POST /api/v1/plans`, `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id`, and `PATCH /api/v1/plans/:id/status`.
- All rate limiters are mounted after `authenticate` and `authorize` middlewares.

### Test Coverage (19 suites, 357 tests)

- **New Test Suites:**
  - `tests/integration/membership.create.test.js` (10 tests): RBAC, trimmed name, duplicate 409 `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`, validation, 405 method not allowed.
  - `tests/integration/membership.list.test.js` (7 tests): RBAC (Coach read allowed, Member 403), exact `planCount` derivation, plan mapping via `toMembershipPlanResponse`, empty list.
- **Extended Test Suites:**
  - `tests/unit/membershipPlan.mapper.test.js`: new fields, nested membership object, standalone null, joiningFee Decimal-to-number.
  - `tests/unit/membershipPlan.validation.test.js`: 3 modes, mutual exclusivity, helper field stripping, periodMonths allowed values.
  - `tests/unit/plan.service.test.js`: 3 modes, transaction rollback on failure, membershipId reassign.
  - `tests/unit/plan.update.service.test.js` & `tests/unit/plan.update.validation.test.js`: membershipId reassign and standalone null, joiningFee rejected.
  - `tests/integration/plan.create.test.js`: Mode 1 (existing), Mode 2 (atomic creation with rollback), Mode 3 (standalone), invalid membership 422.
  - `tests/integration/plan.update.test.js`: reassign membershipId, set standalone null, reject unknown membership 422, reject joiningFee 422.
  - `tests/integration/plan.get.test.js`: full plan includes membershipId, membership, term, recurring, periodMonths, joiningFee, creditNote alias.
  - `tests/integration/plan.version-trigger.test.js`: verified non-commercial columns do not bump plan version.
  - `tests/integration/membershipPlan.seed.test.js`: seed grouping, null-only assignment, rerun idempotency.

## 23. SLAPP-79 / SLAPP-80 — Cafe / Refuel Bar Catalogue & Seed

Implemented the Cafe / Refuel Bar Catalogue API and Glofox Store seed script, per SLAPP-79 and SLAPP-80.

### SLAPP-79/81 Inventory Foundations (sub-task 1/6, 2026-11-03)

- `src/constants/cafe.js`: exported `LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD` 15 and frozen `CafeStockStatus` with uppercase OUT_OF_STOCK / LOW_STOCK / IN_STOCK values. Approved quantity bands are 0 / 1-14 / >=15; status calculation will be implemented in the next sub-task.
- `src/constants/errors.js` and `messages.js`: defined 409 `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED`, `ITEM_STOCK_UPDATED_SUCCESS` and `ITEM_STOCK_STATUS_INVALID`; stock validation reuses `ITEM_STOCK_INVALID`.
- `src/validators/cafe.validation.js`: exported `updateItemStockSchema`, `listInventoryQuerySchema`, `validateUpdateItemStock`, `validateListInventoryQuery`. Stock quantity is required, numeric, integer and >=0, without string coercion. Inventory query extends `listItemsQuerySchema`, reusing page/limit/search/category validation and defaults; status accepts only the three uppercase values. Middleware stores parsed values on `req.validatedBody` / `req.validatedQuery` and uses the existing 422 `VALIDATION_ERROR` responder; unknown fields are stripped.
- Scope: foundations only; the 11 catalogue operations, their responses and the database schema are unchanged. Stock, inventory list and summary endpoints are pending subsequent sub-tasks.
- Verification: `npx jest --runInBand --runTestsByPath tests/unit/cafe.inventory.validation.test.js` (49 passed), `npx jest --runInBand cafe` (8 suites / 135 tests passed), `npm run lint` clean. The full cafe run's best-effort global stale-data sweep was skipped with `PrismaClientInitializationError`; all cafe integration tests passed. No full project test rerun.

### SLAPP-79/81 Data & Business Logic (sub-task 2/6, 2026-11-03)

- Repository: `src/repositories/cafeInventory.repository.js` adds tracked-only `listTrackedItems`, `countTrackedItems`, `getSummaryItems`, and reuses the existing item `update` as `updateStock`. Category and literal, escaped, case-insensitive name/description search are database predicates; public and staff_only rows are both included. Inventory projection contains id, name, categoryId, category id/name, stockQuantity and unitCost; summary selects only stockQuantity and unitCost. Name ASC / id ASC gives stable paging.
- Mappers: `toCafeInventoryItemResponse(item, stockStatus)` returns id, name, categoryId, category id/name when loaded, quantity, unitCost and uppercase stockStatus; unitCost 0 maps to null through `decimalToNumber`. `toCafeStockUpdateResponse` reuses that view and adds lowercase availability plus updatedAt. Both return null for absent records. The catalogue mapper remains unchanged.
- Service: `deriveStockStatus` applies the approved 0 / 1-14 / >=15 bands. `updateStock({ itemId, payload })` uses existing `findById`, rejects untracked items with 409 ITEM_NOT_TRACKED and missing items with 404 ITEM_NOT_FOUND, then executes one existing Prisma update of stockQuantity (and SOLD_OUT in the same write at zero). Positive stock does not write availability. A P2025 race maps to ITEM_NOT_FOUND; other failures propagate. The response uses the persisted row.
- Listing: `listInventory({ query })` returns data/pagination using the existing defaults (page 1, limit 20). Without status it uses DB pagination and a parallel matching count. With status it loads all category/search-matching tracked candidates, derives/filters statuses in the service, then slices the page and computes totals from the filtered set. LOW_STOCK filtering excludes zero, which belongs to OUT_OF_STOCK; the summary low-stock card includes zero. Empty and past-end pages retain correct totals. This full-candidate status path is appropriate for the current small catalogue and scales with matching tracked rows.
- Summary: `getSummary()` returns trackedItems, lowStock (quantity <15 including zero), outOfStock (quantity 0), stockValueAed (AED; named `stockValue` until sub-task 5), itemsMissingCost (unitCost 0), itemsSold null, revenueFromItemsAed null, bestSeller null and salesDataAvailable false. Positive persisted Decimal costs are converted to integer fils using local MINOR_UNITS_PER_AED 100; BigInt multiplication and accumulation avoid floating-point money summation, followed by final Decimal/decimalToNumber conversion to numeric AED. Zero-cost items contribute no value; positive-cost zero-stock items contribute zero without counting as missing cost. Empty tracked data returns zero inventory fields and null sales cards.
- Scope: data/business logic only; HTTP auth, limiters and 405 handlers will be wired in the endpoint sub-tasks. No routes, migrations or catalogue behavior changes.

All 4 cafe unit suites / 97 tests passed. Lint, formatting and diff checks passed; full project suite not re-run. The global stale-data sweep did not report a warning in this run.

### SLAPP-79/81 Stock Endpoint (sub-task 3/6, 2026-11-05)

- Route: `PATCH /api/v1/cafe/items/:itemId/stock` in `src/routes/cafe.routes.js`, registered before `/items/:itemId`: `authenticate` → `authorize(OWNER, ADMIN)` → `cafeLimiter` → existing `validateItemIdParam` → `validateUpdateItemStock` → `cafeInventory.controller.patchItemStock`. `router.all` answers every other method with 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` and `Allow: PATCH`.
- Controller: new `src/controllers/cafeInventory.controller.js` (stock handler only). It passes `req.params.itemId` and `req.validatedBody` to the existing `cafeInventory.service.updateStock`, which already maps the persisted row through `toCafeStockUpdateResponse`, and responds 200 with `ITEM_STOCK_UPDATED_SUCCESS`.
- Stock rules (unchanged from sub-task 2): body `{ quantity }` required integer >= 0 (strings, decimals, negatives and a missing value -> 422 `VALIDATION_ERROR`; a non-UUID id -> 422); direct replace, not increment; quantity 0 sets `SOLD_OUT` in the same write; restocking keeps the current availability (a sold-out item stays `sold_out`); missing item 404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`; untracked item 409 `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED` with no write. Roles are checked before validation, so COACH/MEMBER get 403 whatever the body.
- Response `data`: id, name, categoryId, category { id, name }, quantity, unitCost (0 -> null), stockStatus (OUT_OF_STOCK / LOW_STOCK / IN_STOCK), lowercase availability, updatedAt.
- OpenAPI: added `/api/v1/cafe/items/{itemId}/stock` (`updateCafeItemStock` PATCH with 200/401/403/404/409/422/429 and `cafeItemStockMethodNotAllowed` PUT 405 `Allow: PATCH`), response `CafeItemNotTracked`, schemas `CafeStockStatus` (shared with sub-tasks 4-5), `UpdateCafeItemStockRequest`, `CafeItemStockUpdate`. No test compares routes with the OpenAPI file; the YAML was parsed and all new `$ref`s resolve.

- Ticket text vs implementation: the ticket wording describes three stock levels as Low (<= 6), Average and Full (>= 90). The implemented, approved rule is a single `LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD` of 15 with three statuses: 0 `OUT_OF_STOCK`, 1-14 `LOW_STOCK`, >= 15 `IN_STOCK`. There is no "Average" or "Full" status, no 6 or 90 boundary, and zero stock is its own status; the summary `lowStock` card counts quantity < 15 including zero. Changing to the ticket bands would be a new decision (constants, validator enum, OpenAPI `CafeStockStatus`, mapper/service and tests), not a sync fix.
- Scope: documentation only. No code, test, migration, catalogue or endpoint behaviour change.

### Architecture & Fixed Decisions

- **Base Path & Scope:** Mounted at `/api/v1/cafe`. Provides complete category and item management for both the web admin/catalogue (Next.js) and the native mobile app (Kotlin).
- **RBAC:**
  - Reads (`GET`): `OWNER`, `ADMIN`, `COACH`, `MEMBER`.
  - Writes (`POST`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`, Upload token): `OWNER`, `ADMIN` only. `COACH` is read-only. `MEMBER` gets 403 on write attempts.
- **Member Filtering:**
  - Members only ever see `visibility = 'public'` items across `GET /items`, `GET /items/:itemId` (returns 404 `ITEM_NOT_FOUND` if staff_only), and `GET /categories` (`itemCount` counts public items only for members).
  - Sold-out items (`availability = 'sold_out'`) remain visible to members so they know what is temporarily out of stock.
  - Staff (`OWNER`, `ADMIN`, `COACH`) see all items (`public` and `staff_only`).
- **Rate Limiting:**
  - Every endpoint has a rate limiter placed after authentication/authorization.
  - Reads use `cafeReadLimiter`, writes and uploads use `cafeLimiter`. Both are `createPlanLimiter()` instances (separate counters, keyed by `userId_ip`) with the default limits from `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS` / `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` (15 min / 1000; 100000 in the test env unless `RATE_LIMIT_MAX` is set). There is no cafe-specific 100 / 30 limit in the code.
- **Deletion Rules:**
  - Categories: `DELETE /api/v1/cafe/categories/:categoryId` has a strict deletion guard that checks for associated items; if any item belongs to the category, it returns 409 `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS`.
  - Items: Items are NEVER hard-deleted via API. `DELETE /api/v1/cafe/items/:itemId` and `DELETE /api/v1/cafe/items` return 405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` (`Allow: GET, PATCH` on the item, `Allow: GET, POST` on the collection). Catalogue lifecycle is managed via `availability` and `visibility`.
- **Catalogue Sold-Out & Stock Business Logic (`cafeItem.service.js`):**
  - Tracked items (`isTracked = true`): require `stockQuantity` (int >= 0) and `unitCost` (decimal >= 0). When `stockQuantity` reaches 0 (at creation or via PATCH), availability is automatically set to `sold_out`.
  - Attempting to set `availability = 'available'` when `stockQuantity === 0` is rejected with 400 `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`.
  - Untracked items (`isTracked = false`, made-to-order items like Coffee, Protein Shakes, Protein Coolers): `stockQuantity` and `unitCost` are silently forced to `null`. Availability is managed manually via `PATCH /items/:itemId/availability`.
  - Transitioning from untracked to tracked (`isTracked: false -> true`) without supplying valid `stockQuantity` and `unitCost` returns 400 `ITEM_TRACKED_STOCK_REQUIRED`.
- **Enums & Response Formatting:**
  - Database enums: `CafeItemAvailability` (`AVAILABLE`, `SOLD_OUT`) and `CafeItemVisibility` (`PUBLIC`, `STAFF_ONLY`).
  - API enums: lowercase (`available`, `sold_out`, `public`, `staff_only`). Mapped bidirectionally in `src/repositories/cafe.mapper.js`.
  - Prices: `priceAed` and `unitCost` stored as `DECIMAL(10, 2)` in Postgres; mapped to JS numbers in responses.

| `PATCH`  | `/api/v1/cafe/items/:itemId/stock`        | `OWNER`, `ADMIN`                    | `cafeLimiter`     | `Allow: PATCH`                                                                                        | ✅ 200 `{ data }` stock view, 404 / 409 / 422         |

### Image Upload Token (`POST /api/v1/cafe/uploads/image/token`)

- Uses client-token direct upload pattern via `@vercel/blob` helper `createImageUploadToken(contentType, 'cafe-items')`.
- Accepts `{ contentType: 'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp' }` (unsupported type -> 422 envelope code `VALIDATION_ERROR` with the field message "Image must be a JPEG, PNG or WebP file" in `errors[]`; `IMAGE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED` is not an envelope code on this route).
- Returns identical envelope shape to profile image token: `{ clientToken, expiresAt, maxSizeBytes: 4194304, allowedContentTypes, upload: { method: 'PUT', url, headers } }`.

### Glofox Store Seed (`prisma/seeds/cafe.seed.js`)

- **Command:** `npm run seed:cafe` (defined in `package.json`).
- **Flags:**
  - `--dry-run`: parses sheet and calculates all planned categories/items without database writes.
  - `--force`: overwrites existing items by `externalRef` (default skips existing items, preserving manual admin adjustments to stock, price, availability, and visibility).
- **Source:** Sheet `"Store"` of `docs/Spirit_Level_Glofox_Business_Data.xlsx`.
- **Seed Notes:**
  - `unitCost` is set to `0.00` placeholder (unit purchase cost was not tracked in Glofox export).
  - Seeded stock quantities reflect inventory counts as of extraction on 6 Oct 2026.
  - STYRKR comma-separated category mapped cleanly to `"Supplements"`.
  - Whitespace normalized (trimmed and collapsed repeated whitespace).
- **Seeded Stats:** 61 items, 12 categories, 18 untracked (Coffee, Protein Shakes, Protein Coolers), 43 tracked (23 sold out, 20 available), 7 staff_only items, 61 image URLs present (all https).

### Data Model & Migration

- Migration `20261102130000_add_cafe_catalogue` + reversible `down.sql`:
  - `cafe_categories`: `id` (UUID PK), `name` (unique case-insensitive index on `LOWER(name)`), `description`, timestamps.
  - `cafe_items`: `id` (UUID PK), `externalRef` (unique, nullable, Glofox variant ID), `name`, `categoryId` (FK to `cafe_categories`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT`), `description`, `nutrition` (JSONB), `priceAed` (`DECIMAL(10,2)`, CHECK > 0), `imageUrl`, `availability` (`CafeItemAvailability`, default `AVAILABLE`), `visibility` (`CafeItemVisibility`, default `PUBLIC`), `isTracked` (BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE), `stockQuantity` (INT, nullable, CHECK NULL or >= 0), `unitCost` (`DECIMAL(10,2)`, nullable, CHECK NULL or >= 0), timestamps.
  - CHECK constraints (4): `cafe_items_price_aed_check`, `cafe_items_stock_quantity_check`, `cafe_items_unit_cost_check`, `cafe_items_tracked_consistency_check` (`isTracked = false` ⇒ `stockQuantity` and `unitCost` both NULL; `isTracked = true` ⇒ both NOT NULL). No other CHECK (no stock-0 ⇒ sold-out constraint).
  - Indexes: `cafe_items_categoryId_idx`, `cafe_items_visibility_idx`, `cafe_items_availability_idx`, `cafe_items_visibility_availability_idx`.
  - `down.sql` drops the FK, both tables and both enums.

### Cafe error codes (`src/constants/errors.js`, inventory foundations added 2026-11-03)

Envelope: `{ success: false, message, code, errors? }`. The `AppErrors` key and the wire `code` differ only for `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND_BODY` (wire code is `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`).

| Code (wire)                      | HTTP | Thrown when                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------- | ---- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`             | 404  | `PATCH` / `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` with an unknown id (`AppErrors.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND` (BODY)      | 422  | `POST /items` or `PATCH /items/:itemId` whose body `categoryId` does not exist (`AppErrors.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND_BODY`, same wire code as the 404)                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CATEGORY_EXISTS`                | 409  | `POST` / `PATCH` category with a name another category already has (case-insensitive; also on the DB unique-index race)                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CATEGORY_HAS_ITEMS`             | 409  | `DELETE /categories/:categoryId` while any item (any visibility) references the category                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ITEM_NOT_FOUND`                 | 404  | `GET`, `PATCH`, `PATCH .../availability`, `PATCH .../visibility` with an unknown id; also `GET /items/:itemId` by a MEMBER for a `staff_only` item                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ITEM_NOT_TRACKED`               | 409  | SLAPP-79/81 `cafeInventory.service.updateStock` rejects an existing item with isTracked false; returned by `PATCH /items/:itemId/stock` since sub-task 3 (nothing is written)                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ITEM_SOLD_OUT_ZERO_STOCK`       | 400  | `POST /items` with `availability: available` and tracked stock 0; `PATCH .../availability` to `available` on a tracked item with stock 0                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ITEM_TRACKED_STOCK_REQUIRED`    | 400  | `POST /items` tracked without `stockQuantity` / `unitCost`; `PATCH` switching untracked -> tracked without both; `PATCH` setting a tracked item's stock or cost to `null`                                                                                                                                                         |
| `IMAGE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED`         | 400  | `createImageUploadToken` (storage layer) given an unsupported type. On `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` the validator rejects first with 422 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (field message)                                                                                                                                                   |
| `IMAGE_UPLOAD_NOT_CONFIGURED`    | 503  | `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` while Blob storage is not configured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `IMAGE_UPLOAD_TOKEN_FAILED`      | 502  | `POST /cafe/uploads/image/token` when signing the client token fails. The wire code comes from `blob.storage.js` (`createAppError(502, …, 'IMAGE_UPLOAD_TOKEN_FAILED')`); `AppErrors.IMAGE_UPLOAD_FAILED` (wire code `IMAGE_UPLOAD_FAILED`) is defined in `errors.js` but not used anywhere                                       |
| `VALIDATION_ERROR`               | 422  | Any cafe body / query / path validation failure via `sendValidationFailed` (all failing fields in `errors[]`). Includes the field message `ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE` text when `PATCH /items/:itemId` receives `availability` or `visibility`. Note `AppErrors.VALIDATION_ERROR` itself is 400; cafe routes always answer 422 |
| `UNAUTHORIZED` / `TOKEN_EXPIRED` | 401  | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FORBIDDEN`                      | 403  | Role not allowed (COACH / MEMBER on writes; roles are checked before validation)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS`              | 429  | `cafeReadLimiter` (reads) or `cafeLimiter` (writes / upload token) exhausted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`             | 405  | Any other method on a cafe route, with `Allow` set per route (see the audit table above)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

`ITEM_FIELD_NOT_EDITABLE_HERE` is a `Messages` key (field-level message in `errors[]`), not an `AppErrors` entry and not an envelope `code`.

### OpenAPI (`docs/openapi.yaml`, tag `Cafe`)

- All 11 catalogue operations are documented (categories 4, items 6, upload token 1), plus SLAPP-79/81 `PATCH /items/{itemId}/stock` (`updateCafeItemStock`, with `CafeStockStatus`, `UpdateCafeItemStockRequest`, `CafeItemStockUpdate`, `CafeItemNotTracked`) and `GET /inventory` (`listCafeInventory`, with `CafeInventoryItem`, `CafeMethodNotAllowedGetOnly`) and `GET /inventory/summary` (`getCafeInventorySummary`, with `CafeInventorySummary`), plus a representative 405 operation per route (documented under `put` / `delete`) carrying the correct `Allow` header. `DELETE /items/:itemId` appears only as a 405.
- New components: parameters `CafeCategoryId` / `CafeItemId`; headers `CafeCategoryLocation` / `CafeItemLocation`; responses `ValidationFailed` (it was referenced by the Classes section but never defined), `CafeCategoryNotFound`, `CafeCategoryExists`, `CafeItemNotFound`, `CafeStockRuleViolation`, `CafeItemValidationFailed`, `CafeMethodNotAllowed*` (Collection, Category, Item, PatchOnly, PostOnly); schemas `CafeCategory`, `CafeItem`, `CafeItemCategory`, `Create/UpdateCafeCategoryRequest`, `Create/UpdateCafeItemRequest`, `CafeItemAvailability(Request)`, `CafeItemVisibility(Request)`. The upload token endpoint reuses `ProfileImageTokenRequest` / `ProfileImageUploadToken`.
- Known pre-existing gaps (not touched): the Classes section references `#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse`, `#/components/responses/NotFound` and `#/components/responses/Conflict`, which are not defined, and the `Classes` tag is not declared in `tags`.

## 24. Slot upsert fix, eligibility 405 and full-run status (2026-11-03)

Found on the SLAPP-80 branch (which contains the BE-SCH scheduling commit `12bb2917`) and fixed without any schema change or migration:

- **`class.repository.upsertSlot`** used the compound key `classId_dayOfWeek_startTime`, which no longer exists after migration `20261027130000_add_scheduling_fields` replaced the full unique index with the partial `class_slots_active_unique_idx` (`WHERE active = true`) and added NOT NULL `startDate` / `endTime`. It now uses `findFirst({ classId, dayOfWeek, startTime, active: true })`, updates by id, else creates with `startDate` (today at the facility timezone) and `endTime` (`startTime` + `durationMin`). `prisma/seeds/classes.seed.js` passes `durationMin`. Behaviour notes: an inactive slot at the same class/day/time is ignored (a new slot is created); a `startTime + durationMin` past midnight would wrap `endTime` (no seeded slot does: 29 slots, latest start 20:00, none crossing midnight).
- **`src/routes/class.routes.js`**: added `router.all('/:id/eligibility', methodNotAllowed(['PUT']))` (GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE answer 405 with `Allow: PUT`; before, they fell through to 404).
- **`tests/unit/schedule.adapters.mock.test.js`**: the two slot-POST tests used a hard-coded `startDate` of 2026-11-01, which became "in the past"; they now use today + 7 days.
- **Verification:** `jest class.seed class.methods schedule.adapters` 3 suites / 23 tests pass; `jest class schedule` 25 suites / 289 tests pass (623 s); `eslint .` clean; full run 140 suites / 2057 tests, 2055 pass.
- **Open item:** `tests/integration/plan.list.test.js` (2 tests, "lists the full catalogue (60+ plans) within 300 ms plus the network", sort=name and sort=-price) fails on a latency budget against the remote Neon DB (e.g. 1033 ms vs a 910 ms budget); it is a network-timing check, unrelated to these changes, and also failed in the earlier partial run.
- **DB state after the run:** `cafe_items` 61, `cafe_categories` 12; no `test_` rows in cafe tables, classes or membership_plans; no users with `slcls` in the email.
